\---

\# 1. Purpose

C1 menetapkan \*\*Governance Constitution\*\* bagi PD-03 Governance &

Compliance.

Jika:

\- \*\*Part A\*\* menetapkan identity, strategic purpose, mandate,

organizational boundary, dan strategic direction;

\- \*\*Part B\*\* menetapkan organizational structure;

\- \*\*Part C\*\* menetapkan bagaimana governance dijalankan;

maka C1 menetapkan fondasi konstitusional yang mengatur bagaimana

Governance & Compliance menjalankan mandate governance secara

konsisten, terdokumentasi, dapat ditelusuri, dan dapat diaudit.

C1 menjadi parent constitution bagi Governance Architecture

PD-03.

C1 tidak menggantikan detail yang menjadi tanggung jawab:

\- C2 — Decision Governance;

\- C3 — Delegation Governance;

\- C4 — Accountability Governance;

\- C5 — Authority Governance;

\- C6 — Review Governance;

\- C7 — Escalation Governance;

\- C8 — Cross-Platform Governance;

\- C9–C10 apabila source baseline menetapkan section tersebut.

\---

\# 2. Governance Constitution

Governance Constitution merupakan kontrak governance resmi PD-03.

Konstitusi ini menetapkan bahwa governance PD-03 harus:

\- memiliki authority yang jelas;

\- memiliki ownership yang terdokumentasi;

\- menjaga Domain Boundary;

\- menjaga alignment terhadap Architecture Baseline;

\- menjaga accountability;

\- dapat ditelusuri;

\- dapat diaudit;

\- mendukung controlled organizational evolution.

Tidak diperbolehkan terdapat governance mechanism yang secara

material bertentangan dengan Governance Constitution ini.

\---

\# 3. Governance Domain

PD-03 Governance & Compliance memiliki domain:

\> \*\*Governance & Compliance\*\*

Authority level:

\> \*\*Governance Authority\*\*

Responsibility context yang tersedia pada baseline meliputi:

\- Policy;

\- Standards;

\- Approval;

\- Certification;

\- Compliance.

Responsibility tersebut menjadi domain context bagi Governance

Architecture.

Detail ownership, decision rights, delegation, review, dan

escalation diturunkan pada section governance yang sesuai.

\---

\# 4. Governance Mission

Governance Constitution dibangun untuk memastikan PD-03 mampu:

\- menjaga governance integrity;

\- mengelola governance framework;

\- menjaga policy dan standards governance;

\- mendukung approval governance;

\- mendukung certification governance;

\- menjaga compliance governance;

\- memastikan governance activity dapat ditelusuri;

\- menjaga organizational boundary;

\- mendukung controlled evolution.

Governance bukan bertujuan mengambil alih execution domain Platform

lain.

Governance mengarahkan, mengendalikan, memvalidasi, dan memastikan

kepatuhan sesuai authority yang sah.

\---

\# 5. Governance Philosophy

Governance PD-03 mengikuti prinsip berikut.

\## GP-01 — Governance Enables Controlled Execution

Governance menyediakan aturan, boundary, control, dan assurance

yang memungkinkan organisasi beroperasi secara terkendali.

Governance tidak menjadi operational executor.

\---

\## GP-02 — Authority Before Governance Action

Material governance action harus memiliki constitutional basis dan

authority yang sah.

Tidak boleh terdapat governance action yang memperoleh authority

hanya karena posisi organisasi.

\---

\## GP-03 — Ownership Before Control

Governance control tidak memindahkan ownership domain.

Model:

\`\`\`text

Domain Owner

↓

Owns Domain Responsibility

Governance

↓

Governs Applicable Requirement

⸻

GP-04 — Evidence-Based Governance

Material governance conclusion harus dapat ditelusuri kepada evidence yang relevan.

Governance tidak boleh membuat conclusion material tanpa basis evidence yang memadai.

⸻

GP-05 — Traceable Governance

Governance action harus dapat ditelusuri:

Requirement

↓

Governance Action

↓

Decision / Finding

↓

Outcome

↓

Evidence

⸻

GP-06 — Controlled Evolution

Perubahan governance harus dilakukan melalui mechanism yang sah.

Governance tidak boleh berubah melalui informal operational practice.

⸻

GP-07 — Boundary Preservation

Governance tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengambil alih:

• architecture ownership;

• operational execution;

• technical implementation;

• domain capability ownership;

• Platform ownership lain.

⸻

6\. Governance Objectives

C1 memiliki tujuan governance berikut.

GC-01 — Governance Integrity

Menjaga governance mechanism tetap konsisten dengan constitutional architecture.

⸻

GC-02 — Policy Integrity

Menjaga policy berada dalam lifecycle governance yang jelas.

⸻

GC-03 — Standards Integrity

Menjaga standards dapat ditetapkan, dipelihara, diterapkan, dan ditinjau melalui governance mechanism yang sah.

⸻

GC-04 — Approval Integrity

Memastikan approval dilakukan oleh authority yang tepat dan dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

GC-05 — Certification Integrity

Memastikan certification memiliki basis, scope, evidence, dan authority yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

⸻

GC-06 — Compliance Integrity

Memastikan compliance dapat dinilai, dibuktikan, ditindaklanjuti, dan ditinjau.

⸻

GC-07 — Accountability Clarity

Memastikan governance action memiliki ownership dan accountability yang jelas.

⸻

GC-08 — Governance Traceability

Memastikan governance activity dapat ditelusuri sepanjang lifecycle-nya.

⸻

C1 mencakup constitutional governance terhadap domain PD-03.

8\. Out of Scope

C1 tidak mengambil primary ownership terhadap:

• enterprise strategic direction;

• architecture implementation;

• runtime execution;

• AI engineering implementation;

• infrastructure execution;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

• developer execution;

• business operation;

• capability execution milik Platform lain.

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance dependency terhadap domain tersebut.

Dependency tidak memindahkan ownership.

14\. Governance and Delegation

Governance delegation harus berasal dari authority yang sah.

Delegation tidak boleh:

• memindahkan organizational ownership;

• menghapus accountability;

• memperluas authority tanpa basis;

• menciptakan authority baru.

Detail delegation ditetapkan pada C3.

⸻

15\. Governance and Accountability

Setiap governance action harus memiliki accountability context.

C1 tidak menggantikan C4.

Relationship:

Governance Action

↓

Accountability Context

↓

C4 Accountability Governance

Accountability harus tetap dapat ditelusuri meskipun action didelegasikan.

⸻

16\. Governance and Review

Governance review digunakan untuk memastikan governance mechanism tetap efektif dan compliant.

C1 menetapkan constitutional requirement.

C6 menetapkan review mechanism secara detail.

Review harus mempertimbangkan:

• evidence;

• criteria;

• authority;

• scope;

• finding;

• condition;

• outcome.

⸻

17\. Governance and Escalation

Governance matter yang tidak dapat diselesaikan pada authority level yang berlaku harus menggunakan escalation mechanism.

C7 menjadi child governance architecture untuk escalation.

Model:

Governance Finding

↓

Local Resolution

↓

Authority Check

↓

Escalation if Required

↓

Applicable Authority

Escalation tidak boleh menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

18\. Governance and Cross-Platform Relationship

PD-03 dapat berinteraksi dengan Platform lain ketika governance requirement atau compliance matter memiliki cross-platform impact.

Relationship:

PD-03 Governance

│

├── Policy Interface

├── Standards Interface

├── Approval Interface

├── Certification Interface

└── Compliance Interface

Cross-platform interaction tidak berarti PD-03 mengambil ownership domain Platform lain.

⸻

19\. Policy Governance

Policy merupakan salah satu responsibility context PD-03.

Policy governance harus mempertahankan:

• purpose;

• scope;

• owner;

• authority;

• effective status;

• applicability;

• review cycle;

• change history.

Policy tidak boleh dibuat tanpa identified owner dan authority.

⸻

20\. Standards Governance

Standards governance memastikan standards:

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki owner;

• memiliki applicability;

• memiliki lifecycle;

• dapat direview;

• dapat diperbarui melalui approved mechanism.

Standards tidak boleh digunakan untuk menciptakan authority yang tidak memiliki constitutional basis.

⸻

21\. Approval Governance

Approval harus memiliki:

• subject;

• scope;

• approver;

• authority basis;

• evidence;

• decision record;

• effective status.

Approval authority ditentukan oleh applicable authority mechanism.

Governance requirement tidak otomatis memberikan approval right kepada setiap Governance function.

⸻

22\. Certification Governance

Certification harus memiliki:

• certification scope;

• applicable criteria;

• evidence;

• assessor/reviewer;

• authority;

• result;

• validity;

• review/renewal condition.

Certification tidak boleh dianggap sebagai technical implementation ownership.

⸻

23\. Compliance Governance

Compliance governance memastikan bahwa applicable requirement:

Requirement

↓

Applicability

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Finding

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Verification

↓

Closure

Compliance tidak berhenti pada status pass/fail.

Material finding harus memiliki disposition yang dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

24\. Governance Evidence

Material governance activity harus mempertahankan evidence yang cukup untuk mendukung:

• claim;

• decision;

• approval;

• certification;

• compliance assessment;

• finding;

• corrective action;

• closure.

Evidence harus mempertahankan:

• source;

• timestamp;

• owner;

• status;

• version;

• relationship to action.

⸻

25\. Governance Record Integrity

Governance records harus:

• versioned;

• attributable;

• traceable;

• reviewable;

• auditable.

Historical governance evidence tidak boleh diubah untuk menghilangkan history.

Jika koreksi diperlukan, correction harus menghasilkan traceable record.

⸻

Governance lifecycle PD-03:

Define

↓

Approve

↓

Publish

↓

Apply

↓

Monitor

↓

Review

↓

Improve

↓

Retire / Replace

Lifecycle detail dapat diturunkan pada child governance sections.

⸻

27\. Governance Control Model

Governance control dapat berbentuk:

• preventive control;

• detective control;

• corrective control;

• assurance control.

Control harus memiliki:

• purpose;

• owner;

• applicability;

• evidence;

• review mechanism.

Control tidak boleh dibuat tanpa responsibility owner.

⸻

28\. Governance Non-Compliance

Jika terdapat governance non-compliance:

Identify

↓

Record

↓

Assess

↓

Classify

↓

Assign Owner

↓

Correct

↓

Verify

↓

Close

Jika matter tidak dapat diselesaikan dalam authority yang tersedia, gunakan escalation mechanism.

⸻

29\. Governance Change Control

Perubahan terhadap Governance Constitution harus:

1\. memiliki change proposal;

2\. memiliki rationale;

3\. memiliki impact assessment;

4\. memiliki authority review;

5\. memiliki decision record;

6\. memiliki effective state;

7\. dapat ditelusuri.

Tidak diperbolehkan mengubah constitutional governance melalui informal convention.

⸻

30\. Governance Independence

Governance review harus menjaga independence yang memadai.

Pihak yang:

• memiliki material conflict of interest;

• menjadi subject langsung dari finding;

• memiliki incentive yang dapat mempengaruhi conclusion;

harus ditangani sesuai applicable review governance.

Independence tidak berarti seluruh review harus dilakukan oleh pihak eksternal.

Independence berarti conclusion tidak boleh dikendalikan secara tidak semestinya oleh subject yang sedang dinilai.

⸻

Interface tidak otomatis berarti organizational hierarchy.

⸻

32\. Governance Ownership Principle

Canonical principle:

Governance responsibility does not automatically transfer domain ownership.

Jika PD-03 menetapkan governance requirement terhadap Platform lain:

PD-03

Governance Requirement

↓

Applicable Platform

↓

Domain Owner

↓

Execution / Remediation

Domain owner tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap domain execution.

⸻

33\. Governance and Platform Autonomy

Setiap Platform tetap mempertahankan autonomy dalam domainnya.

PD-03 memastikan governance compliance tanpa:

• mengambil alih execution;

• menggantikan domain owner;

• membuat architecture decision;

• menjalankan technical implementation;

• mengambil alih operational management.

⸻

34\. Governance Conflict Model

Jika terjadi konflik:

Identify Conflict

↓

Classify Domain

↓

Identify Applicable Authority

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve Locally if Possible

↓

Escalate if Required

↓

Record Outcome

Conflict tidak boleh diselesaikan berdasarkan organizational preference semata.

Applicable authority dan domain boundary harus menjadi basis.

⸻

35\. Governance Traceability Model

Setiap material governance matter harus dapat ditelusuri:

Requirement

↓

Policy / Standard

↓

Governance Action

↓

Decision / Approval

↓

Evidence

↓

Finding / Outcome

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Verification

Traceability merupakan constitutional requirement.

⸻

36\. Relationship to Operating Architecture

Part D menentukan bagaimana PD-03 menjalankan operasi.

C1 menentukan governance boundary dan control.

Relationship:

Governance Constitution

↓

Operating Constitution

↓

Operational Execution

Operating mechanism tidak boleh bypass governance requirement.

Namun governance tidak boleh mengambil alih operational execution.

⸻

37\. Relationship to Performance Architecture

Performance Architecture mengukur hasil dan effectiveness.

Governance dapat menjadi input terhadap performance evaluation.

Namun:

Governance

≠

Performance Measurement

Governance menetapkan control dan accountability.

Performance architecture mengukur outcome.

⸻

39\. Governance Success Conditions

C1 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. governance memiliki constitutional basis;

2\. governance authority jelas;

3\. policy responsibility jelas;

4\. standards responsibility jelas;

5\. approval mechanism memiliki authority basis;

6\. certification memiliki evidence basis;

7\. compliance dapat diverifikasi;

8\. governance action dapat ditelusuri;

9\. governance record dapat diaudit;

10\. ownership domain tetap terjaga;

11\. architecture authority tidak diambil alih;

12\. operational execution tidak diambil alih;

13\. governance conflicts memiliki resolution path;

14\. material findings dapat dieskalasikan;

15\. governance dapat berevolusi melalui controlled change;

16\. C2–C8 memiliki constitutional parent yang jelas;

17\. C9–C10 hanya dikonstruksi setelah source resolution.

⸻

40\. Canonical Governance Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance governs policy, standards, approval, certification, and compliance within its authorized Governance Authority domain through explicit ownership, evidence-based controls, traceable decisions, accountable review, controlled escalation, and governed evolution, while preserving Architecture Authority, operational ownership, and the autonomy of other Platform Divisions.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance menjalankan governance atas policy, standards, approval, certification, dan compliance dalam domain Governance Authority melalui ownership yang eksplisit, control berbasis evidence, decision yang dapat ditelusuri, review yang accountable, escalation yang terkendali, dan evolusi governance yang sah, dengan tetap menjaga Architecture Authority, operational ownership, serta autonomy Platform Division lainnya.

⸻

43\. Canonical Status

C1 — Governance Constitution ditetapkan sebagai:

Frozen Constitutional Parent for PD-03 Governance Architecture

C1 menjadi dasar bagi:

• Decision Governance;

• Delegation Governance;

• Accountability Governance;

• Authority Governance;

• Review Governance;

• Escalation Governance;

• Cross-Platform Governance;

• source-defined subsequent governance sections.

C1 tidak menggantikan child governance sections.

Setiap child section harus tetap berada dalam boundary yang ditetapkan oleh C1.

⸻

44\. Construction Rule for Child Sections

Seluruh child governance section wajib:

1\. traceable kepada C1;

2\. memiliki single primary governance concern;

3\. tidak menduplikasi concern child section lain;

4\. tidak menciptakan authority tanpa constitutional basis;

5\. mempertahankan organizational ownership;

6\. mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

7\. memiliki evidence dan traceability;

8\. menjalani Gold Standard Review sebelum freeze.

⸻

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part C — Governance Architecture

\# 1. Purpose

C2 menetapkan bagaimana \*\*architecture decision governance\*\* dikelola

dalam PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

C2 memastikan bahwa architecture decision:

\- memiliki authority yang sah;

\- memiliki Decision Owner;

\- berada dalam applicable Domain Boundary;

\- memiliki governance basis;

\- memiliki evidence yang memadai;

\- selaras dengan Architecture Baseline;

\- dapat ditelusuri;

\- dapat diaudit;

\- memiliki status dan lifecycle yang jelas.

C2 tidak mengambil alih Architecture Authority.

Architecture decision tetap berada pada authority domain yang

ditetapkan oleh AIOS Architecture Governance.

Dalam konteks PD-03:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Governs

Decision Governance

↓

Architecture Decision Process

↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Decision

Dengan demikian:

Decision Governance

≠

Architecture Authority

⸻

2\. Decision Governance Constitution

Architecture Decision Governance merupakan constitutional mechanism yang memastikan architecture decisions dibuat melalui governance yang sah.

Setiap material architecture decision harus:

• memiliki decision scope;

• memiliki authority basis;

• memiliki Decision Owner;

• memiliki rationale;

• memiliki evidence;

• mempertimbangkan impact;

• mengikuti applicable Architecture Baseline;

• mengikuti applicable Governance Constitution;

• memiliki approval state;

• memiliki implementation status;

• memiliki review history.

Tidak diperbolehkan menganggap suatu architecture decision valid hanya karena telah dibuat secara informal.

⸻

3\. Governance Position

PD-03 memiliki responsibility untuk memastikan decision governance berjalan sesuai Governance Constitution.

PD-03 memastikan:

Decision

↓

Authority

↓

Governance

↓

Traceability

↓

Accountability

PD-03 tidak menggantikan:

• Architecture Authority;

• Executive Authority;

• Platform Domain Owner;

• Operational Owner;

• Implementation Owner.

⸻

4\. Architecture Decision Boundary

Architecture decision berada dalam Architecture domain.

Karena itu:

Architecture Concern

↓

Architecture Authority

PD-03 berfungsi pada governance layer:

Governance Concern

↓

Governance Authority

Hubungan kedua domain:

Architecture Authority

│

│ Architecture Decision

▼

Governance Framework

│

│ Governance Validation

▼

Governance Decision / Control

PD-03 tidak menggunakan governance authority untuk mengambil alih architecture decision rights yang diberikan kepada Architecture Authority.

⸻

5\. Decision Governance Objectives

ADG-01 — Decision Authority Integrity

Memastikan setiap architecture decision dibuat oleh authority yang sah.

⸻

ADG-02 — Decision Ownership

Memastikan setiap material architecture decision memiliki Decision Owner yang eksplisit.

⸻

ADG-03 — Decision Traceability

Memastikan decision dapat ditelusuri dari context sampai outcome.

⸻

ADG-04 — Decision Compliance

Memastikan architecture decision mengikuti applicable governance requirements.

⸻

ADG-05 — Architecture Alignment

Memastikan architecture decision mempertimbangkan dan tetap selaras dengan applicable Architecture Baseline.

⸻

ADG-06 — Decision Accountability

Memastikan responsibility terhadap decision dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

ADG-07 — Decision Auditability

Memastikan material decision memiliki record dan evidence yang dapat diaudit.

⸻

ADG-08 — Decision Consistency

Memastikan architecture decision menggunakan decision governance mechanism yang konsisten.

⸻

6\. Decision Principles

Seluruh architecture decision governance mengikuti prinsip berikut.

DP-01 — Authority Before Decision

Architecture decision hanya sah apabila dibuat oleh authority yang memiliki jurisdiction terhadap decision tersebut.

⸻

DP-02 — Ownership Before Approval

Decision Owner harus jelas sebelum approval diberikan.

⸻

DP-03 — Governance Does Not Transfer Architecture Ownership

Governance review atau governance approval tidak secara otomatis memindahkan architecture ownership.

⸻

DP-04 — Evidence Before Material Conclusion

Material architecture decision harus memiliki evidence yang memadai untuk mendukung conclusion.

⸻

DP-05 — Architecture Baseline Alignment

Architecture decision harus dinilai terhadap applicable Architecture Baseline.

⸻

DP-06 — Explicit Decision State

Setiap material decision harus memiliki state yang jelas.

⸻

DP-07 — Traceability by Design

Decision traceability harus dibangun sejak decision dimulai, bukan ditambahkan setelah decision selesai.

⸻

DP-08 — Accountability Preservation

Delegation tidak menghapus accountability dari delegating authority sesuai applicable governance model.

⸻

DP-09 — Boundary Preservation

Decision governance tidak boleh digunakan untuk menciptakan authority di luar Domain Boundary.

⸻

DP-10 — Review Before Material Commitment

Material decision harus melewati review yang sesuai sebelum menjadi effective.

⸻

7\. Decision Scope

C2 mencakup governance terhadap:

• architecture decision;

• architecture decision proposal;

• architecture decision authority;

• decision ownership;

• decision approval;

• decision evidence;

• decision rationale;

• decision impact;

• decision record;

• decision status;

• decision review;

• decision traceability;

• decision auditability;

• decision implementation status;

• decision closure.

⸻

8\. Out of Scope

C2 tidak menjadi primary owner terhadap:

• Architecture Model;

• Architecture Baseline;

• Architecture Decision domain;

• architecture implementation;

• runtime execution;

• AI engineering execution;

• infrastructure execution;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

• operational decision flow;

• enterprise strategic direction.

Concern tersebut tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

9\. Decision Authority Model

Decision authority mengikuti hierarchy authority yang berlaku.

Canonical model:

Enterprise Authority

↓

Platform Authority

↓

Sub Division Authority

↓

Team Authority

↓

Delegated Authority

Authority hanya dapat digunakan dalam Domain Boundary yang ditetapkan.

Authority level tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengambil keputusan di luar domainnya.

⸻

10\. Architecture Decision Authority

Architecture decision authority tetap berada pada applicable Architecture Authority.

PD-03 tidak memperoleh Architecture Decision Authority hanya karena PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.

Relationship:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

│ Governs

▼

Decision Governance

│

│ applies to

▼

Architecture Decision

│

▼

PD-02

Architecture Authority

Jika architecture decision memiliki governance implication, PD-03 dapat melakukan applicable governance review.

Governance review tidak menggantikan architecture decision.

⸻

11\. Decision Owner

Setiap material architecture decision harus memiliki:

Decision Owner

Decision Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• decision context;

• decision rationale;

• decision completeness;

• impact assessment;

• decision record;

• decision outcome.

Decision Owner harus berasal dari authority domain yang sah.

PD-03 tidak secara otomatis menjadi Decision Owner hanya karena menjalankan governance.

⸻

12\. Decision Sponsor

Jika diperlukan, architecture decision dapat memiliki:

Decision Sponsor

Decision Sponsor memberikan:

• strategic context;

• organizational support;

• escalation support;

• resource context.

Decision Sponsor tidak otomatis menjadi Decision Owner.

⸻

13\. Approving Authority

Approving Authority adalah authority yang memiliki hak memberikan approval terhadap decision sesuai decision class.

Approval harus memiliki:

• authority basis;

• scope;

• decision reference;

• approval state;

• effective status.

Approval authority tidak boleh diasumsikan dari organizational seniority semata.

⸻

14\. Governance Reviewer

Governance Reviewer memastikan architecture decision:

• mengikuti applicable governance;

• memiliki authority basis;

• memiliki required evidence;

• tidak melanggar governance constraint;

• memiliki required record;

• memiliki traceability.

Governance Reviewer tidak otomatis menjadi Architecture Decision Authority.

⸻

15\. Architecture Reviewer

Architecture Reviewer menilai:

• architectural consistency;

• Architecture Baseline alignment;

• architecture impact;

• architectural alternatives;

• architectural consequences;

• domain boundary.

Architecture Reviewer bekerja dalam applicable Architecture Authority.

PD-03 tidak menggantikan Architecture Reviewer.

⸻

16\. Decision Custodian

Decision Custodian bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• decision record;

• metadata;

• version;

• archival state;

• retrieval;

• review history.

Decision Custodian tidak memiliki decision right hanya karena mengelola record.

⸻

17\. Decision Roles

Canonical role separation:

￼

Satu individu dapat memegang lebih dari satu role apabila tidak melanggar applicable independence requirement.

⸻

18\. Decision Lifecycle

Architecture decision lifecycle:

Initiated

↓

Drafted

↓

Validated

↓

Impact Assessed

↓

Reviewed

↓

Approved / Rejected

↓

Published

↓

Implemented

↓

Monitored

↓

Reviewed

↓

Closed

↓

Archived

Tidak semua decision harus melalui tingkat lifecycle yang sama.

Decision classification menentukan required governance depth.

⸻

19\. Decision Initiation

Decision dapat dimulai ketika terdapat:

• architecture change proposal;

• architecture conflict;

• new architectural requirement;

• material dependency;

• architectural risk;

• architecture baseline issue;

• domain boundary issue;

• strategic architectural implication.

Initiation tidak berarti decision telah disetujui.

⸻

20\. Decision Intake

Decision intake harus menangkap minimal:

• Decision ID;

• title;

• initiating party;

• decision category;

• decision scope;

• affected domain;

• urgency;

• initial impact;

• required authority;

• related architecture artifact.

Decision intake menjadi entry point untuk governance traceability.

⸻

21\. Decision Classification

Architecture decision harus diklasifikasikan berdasarkan:

• decision domain;

• materiality;

• impact;

• authority level;

• cross-platform effect;

• governance implication;

• implementation consequence.

Classification menentukan:

• reviewer;

• approval path;

• evidence requirement;

• escalation requirement;

• review depth.

⸻

22\. Decision Analysis

Material architecture decision harus mempertimbangkan:

• current state;

• target state;

• problem;

• alternatives;

• constraints;

• dependencies;

• risks;

• benefits;

• consequences;

• reversibility;

• affected boundaries.

Analysis harus dibedakan dari decision.

Analysis

≠

Decision

⸻

23\. Decision Alternatives

Jika terdapat lebih dari satu viable alternative, decision record harus mencatat:

￼

Tidak boleh menyembunyikan material alternative hanya untuk mempercepat approval.

⸻

24\. Decision Rationale

Setiap material decision harus memiliki rationale yang menjelaskan:

• why;

• evidence;

• constraints;

• authority basis;

• selected alternative;

• expected consequence.

Rationale harus tetap dapat ditelusuri setelah decision selesai.

⸻

25\. Decision Impact Assessment

Impact assessment harus mempertimbangkan jika relevan:

• architecture;

• governance;

• organization;

• operation;

• security;

• quality;

• runtime;

• AI engineering;

• infrastructure;

• cross-platform dependency.

Impact assessment tidak otomatis memberikan decision right kepada setiap domain yang terdampak.

⸻

26\. Governance Validation

Governance validation memastikan:

0\. authority jelas;

0\. Decision Owner jelas;

0\. required evidence tersedia;

0\. governance requirements dipenuhi;

0\. decision record lengkap;

0\. required review dilakukan;

0\. applicable Architecture Baseline dipertimbangkan;

0\. conflict dan escalation path tersedia.

⸻

27\. Architecture Validation

Architecture validation tetap dilakukan oleh applicable Architecture Authority.

Validation mencakup:

• architectural correctness;

• Architecture Baseline alignment;

• domain boundary;

• dependency;

• architectural consistency;

• architectural consequences.

PD-03 tidak menggantikan architecture validation.

⸻

28\. Approval Model

Canonical flow:

Decision Proposal

↓

Decision Validation

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Architecture Review

↓

Authority Review

↓

Approval / Rejection

↓

Publication

↓

Implementation Authorization

Tidak semua decision membutuhkan seluruh stage dengan kedalaman yang sama.

⸻

29\. Approval Boundary

Approval harus dibedakan dari implementation authorization.

Architecture Approval

↓

Architectural Validity

tidak otomatis berarti:

Implementation Authorization

Implementation tetap membutuhkan authorization dari applicable implementation owner atau authority.

⸻

30\. Rejection

Architecture decision dapat ditolak apabila:

• authority tidak valid;

• evidence tidak memadai;

• architecture impact tidak dapat diterima;

• governance requirement tidak terpenuhi;

• decision berada di luar scope;

• required review belum selesai;

• material conflict belum diselesaikan.

Rejection harus memiliki rationale.

⸻

31\. Conditional Approval

Jika decision dapat diterima dengan condition:

Decision

↓

Condition

↓

Owner

↓

Due / Review State

↓

Verification

Condition harus:

• explicit;

• owned;

• traceable;

• verifiable.

Conditional approval tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan material unresolved issue.

⸻

32\. Decision Publication

Decision yang telah approved harus dipublikasikan melalui canonical decision mechanism.

Publication harus mempertahankan:

• decision ID;

• status;

• effective date;

• authority;

• scope;

• related artifacts;

• conditions;

• implementation state.

⸻

33\. Decision Implementation Boundary

PD-03 governance tidak menjadi implementation executor.

Setelah decision approved:

Architecture Decision

↓

Implementation Owner

↓

Execution

↓

Implementation Evidence

Implementation Owner tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap execution.

⸻

34\. Decision Monitoring

Material architecture decision harus dapat dimonitor apabila:

• implementation belum selesai;

• condition belum closed;

• deviation muncul;

• expected outcome belum tercapai;

• new evidence muncul;

• architecture baseline berubah.

Monitoring tidak mengubah decision secara otomatis.

⸻

35\. Decision Review

Decision review dapat dipicu oleh:

• scheduled review;

• material architecture change;

• new evidence;

• implementation deviation;

• new dependency;

• governance finding;

• strategic change.

Review menghasilkan salah satu:

Maintain

Condition

Amend

Supersede

Revoke

Escalate

Decision review bukan retroactive rewriting.

⸻

36\. Decision History

Historical decision record harus dipertahankan.

Jika decision berubah:

Decision v1

↓

Review

↓

Decision v2

Jangan mengganti historical record v1 seolah-olah v2 selalu berlaku.

Perubahan harus menghasilkan traceable history.

⸻

37\. Decision Register

Decision Register minimal memiliki:

￼

39\. Decision Auditability

Decision harus dapat diaudit terhadap:

• who;

• what;

• why;

• when;

• authority;

• evidence;

• approval;

• consequence;

• implementation;

• review.

Auditability tidak berarti seluruh decision harus dipublikasikan kepada seluruh organisasi.

Access tetap mengikuti applicable information governance.

⸻

40\. Decision Exceptions

Decision governance exception harus memiliki:

• exception ID;

• reason;

• scope;

• authority;

• duration;

• compensating control;

• review;

• closure.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent bypass.

⸻

41\. Decision Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

0\. authority tidak jelas;

0\. decision berada di luar scope;

0\. authority conflict terjadi;

0\. cross-domain decision tidak dapat diselesaikan;

0\. strategic implication material;

0\. governance conflict material;

0\. unresolved architecture risk material.

Canonical flow:

Decision Issue

↓

Classify

↓

Applicable Authority Check

↓

Local Resolution

↓

Escalation if Required

↓

Applicable Higher / Cross-Domain Authority

Escalation tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

42\. Cross-Platform Decision

Jika architecture decision berdampak pada lebih dari satu Platform:

Architecture Decision

↓

Affected Platforms

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Cross-Platform Review

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision

Affected Platform tetap mempertahankan domain ownership.

Cross-platform decision tidak berarti PD-03 menjadi owner atas Platform yang terdampak.

⸻

43\. Decision Conflict

Jika terdapat conflict:

Identify Domain

↓

Identify Authority Type

↓

Identify Decision Class

↓

Identify Applicable Precedence

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve / Escalate

Conflict tidak boleh diselesaikan berdasarkan organizational preference semata.

⸻

44\. Architecture Decision vs Governance Decision

Keduanya harus dibedakan.

￼

PD-03 mengatur governance mechanism yang memastikan keempatnya berjalan sesuai applicable authority.

⸻

45\. Decision vs Delegation

C2 menetapkan governance terhadap decision.

C3 akan menetapkan delegation governance.

Karena itu C2 hanya menetapkan:

Delegated Authority

↓

Must be traceable

↓

Must remain within scope

Detail delegation lifecycle berada pada C3.

⸻

46\. Decision vs Accountability

Decision Owner tetap accountable terhadap decision sesuai applicable governance model.

Delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus accountability.

Detail accountability governance berada pada C4.

⸻

47\. Decision vs Authority

C2 tidak menggantikan C5.

C5 akan menetapkan detail:

• authority;

• right to act;

• approval;

• veto;

• override;

• delegation;

• authority boundaries.

C2 menggunakan authority model tersebut.

⸻

48\. Decision vs Review

C2 menetapkan bahwa review diperlukan sesuai materiality.

C6 akan menetapkan detailed review governance:

• reviewer;

• review independence;

• finding;

• condition;

• review outcome.

C2 tidak menduplikasi C6.

⸻

49\. Decision Governance Control Model

Decision governance control terdiri dari:

Preventive

↓

Authority Validation

Detective

↓

Decision Review

Corrective

↓

Condition / Remediation

Assurance

↓

Audit / Traceability

Control harus proporsional terhadap decision materiality.

⸻

50\. Decision Materiality

Decision materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• architecture impact;

• number of affected Platforms;

• boundary impact;

• governance impact;

• reversibility;

• security impact;

• operational impact;

• financial/resource implication;

• strategic implication.

Materiality classification harus memiliki basis yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

⸻

51\. Decision Quality Controls

Material architecture decision harus diperiksa terhadap:

DQ-01 — Authority Validity

Apakah decision dibuat dalam authority yang tepat?

DQ-02 — Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup?

DQ-03 — Scope Clarity

Apakah decision scope jelas?

DQ-04 — Alternative Completeness

Apakah material alternatives dipertimbangkan?

DQ-05 — Impact Completeness

Apakah material impacts dinilai?

DQ-06 — Architecture Alignment

Apakah decision aligned dengan Architecture Baseline?

DQ-07 — Governance Compliance

Apakah applicable governance requirement dipenuhi?

DQ-08 — Traceability

Apakah decision dapat ditelusuri?

⸻

52\. Decision Failure States

Decision governance harus dapat menghasilkan state:

Blocked

Rejected

Deferred

Conditioned

Escalated

Superseded

Revoked

Setiap non-success state harus memiliki rationale.

Tidak boleh mengubah status menjadi Approved hanya untuk menghilangkan blocker.

⸻

53\. Governance Independence

Governance review harus memiliki independence yang memadai dari decision subject ketika materiality mengharuskannya.

Reviewer harus mampu menghasilkan conclusion tanpa undue influence dari decision owner.

Independence requirement harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• conflict of interest;

• authority concentration;

• decision impact.

⸻

54\. Decision Record Integrity

Decision record:

• tidak boleh diubah untuk menghapus historical evidence;

• harus mempertahankan version history;

• harus memiliki attributable author;

• harus mempertahankan approval history;

• harus mempertahankan supersession history.

Correction harus menghasilkan traceable correction record.

⸻

55\. Decision Change

Jika architecture decision harus berubah:

Existing Decision

↓

Change Trigger

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Review

↓

New Decision / Amendment

↓

Approval

↓

Publication

Decision change tidak boleh dilakukan melalui silent overwrite.

⸻

Canonical interface:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

├── Governance Requirement

├── Governance Review

├── Compliance Control

└── Decision Governance

│

▼

│

└── Architecture Decision

PD-03 menjaga governance integrity.

PD-02 menjaga architecture integrity.

Keduanya bekerja melalui defined interface.

⸻

57\. Governance-to-Executive Interface

Jika architecture decision memiliki material strategic implication:

Architecture Decision

↓

Strategic Impact

↓

↓

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih Executive Authority.

58\. Governance-to-Platform Interface

Jika architecture decision berdampak pada Platform lain:

Architecture Decision

↓

Affected Platform

↓

Platform Review

↓

Implementation / Response

Platform owner mempertahankan execution ownership.

⸻

59\. Decision Governance Success Conditions

C2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. setiap material architecture decision memiliki authority basis;

0\. Decision Owner jelas;

0\. decision scope jelas;

0\. required architecture review dilakukan;

0\. governance review dilakukan ketika applicable;

0\. Architecture Baseline alignment dapat dibuktikan;

0\. material decision memiliki evidence;

0\. approval dapat ditelusuri;

0\. implementation authorization dibedakan dari architecture approval;

0\. historical decision record dipertahankan;

0\. decision change memiliki lifecycle;

0\. delegation tidak menghilangkan accountability;

0\. cross-platform impact dapat ditelusuri;

0\. authority conflict memiliki escalation path;

0\. PD-03 tidak mengambil alih Architecture Authority;

0\. architecture decision tetap berada pada applicable Architecture Authority;

0\. decision governance dapat diaudit.

⸻

60\. Canonical Decision Governance Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance governs the integrity, authorization, traceability, accountability, and compliance of architecture decision processes while preserving the decision rights and ownership of the applicable Architecture Authority.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance mengatur integritas, kewenangan, keterlacakan, akuntabilitas, dan kepatuhan proses architecture decision dengan tetap mempertahankan decision rights dan ownership pada Architecture Authority yang berlaku.

Architecture Decision

\# 1. Purpose

C2 menetapkan bagaimana \*\*architecture decision governance\*\* dikelola

dalam PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

C2 memastikan bahwa architecture decision:

\- memiliki authority yang sah;

\- memiliki Decision Owner;

\- berada dalam applicable Domain Boundary;

\- memiliki governance basis;

\- memiliki evidence yang memadai;

\- selaras dengan Architecture Baseline;

\- dapat ditelusuri;

\- dapat diaudit;

\- memiliki status dan lifecycle yang jelas.

C2 tidak mengambil alih Architecture Authority.

Architecture decision tetap berada pada authority domain yang

ditetapkan oleh AIOS Architecture Governance.

Dalam konteks PD-03:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Governs

Decision Governance

↓

Architecture Decision Process

↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Decision

Dengan demikian:

Decision Governance

≠

Architecture Authority

⸻

2\. Decision Governance Constitution

Architecture Decision Governance merupakan constitutional mechanism yang memastikan architecture decisions dibuat melalui governance yang sah.

Setiap material architecture decision harus:

• memiliki decision scope;

• memiliki authority basis;

• memiliki Decision Owner;

• memiliki rationale;

• memiliki evidence;

• mempertimbangkan impact;

• mengikuti applicable Architecture Baseline;

• mengikuti applicable Governance Constitution;

• memiliki approval state;

• memiliki implementation status;

• memiliki review history.

Tidak diperbolehkan menganggap suatu architecture decision valid hanya karena telah dibuat secara informal.

⸻

3\. Governance Position

PD-03 memiliki responsibility untuk memastikan decision governance berjalan sesuai Governance Constitution.

PD-03 memastikan:

Decision

↓

Authority

↓

Governance

↓

Traceability

↓

Accountability

PD-03 tidak menggantikan:

• Architecture Authority;

• Executive Authority;

• Platform Domain Owner;

• Operational Owner;

• Implementation Owner.

⸻

4\. Architecture Decision Boundary

Architecture decision berada dalam Architecture domain.

Karena itu:

Architecture Concern

↓

Architecture Authority

PD-03 berfungsi pada governance layer:

Governance Concern

↓

Governance Authority

Hubungan kedua domain:

Architecture Authority

│

│ Architecture Decision

▼

Governance Framework

│

│ Governance Validation

▼

Governance Decision / Control

PD-03 tidak menggunakan governance authority untuk mengambil alih architecture decision rights yang diberikan kepada Architecture Authority.

⸻

5\. Decision Governance Objectives

ADG-01 — Decision Authority Integrity

Memastikan setiap architecture decision dibuat oleh authority yang sah.

⸻

ADG-02 — Decision Ownership

Memastikan setiap material architecture decision memiliki Decision Owner yang eksplisit.

⸻

ADG-03 — Decision Traceability

Memastikan decision dapat ditelusuri dari context sampai outcome.

⸻

ADG-04 — Decision Compliance

Memastikan architecture decision mengikuti applicable governance requirements.

⸻

ADG-05 — Architecture Alignment

Memastikan architecture decision mempertimbangkan dan tetap selaras dengan applicable Architecture Baseline.

⸻

ADG-06 — Decision Accountability

Memastikan responsibility terhadap decision dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

ADG-07 — Decision Auditability

Memastikan material decision memiliki record dan evidence yang dapat diaudit.

⸻

ADG-08 — Decision Consistency

Memastikan architecture decision menggunakan decision governance mechanism yang konsisten.

⸻

6\. Decision Principles

Seluruh architecture decision governance mengikuti prinsip berikut.

DP-01 — Authority Before Decision

Architecture decision hanya sah apabila dibuat oleh authority yang memiliki jurisdiction terhadap decision tersebut.

⸻

DP-02 — Ownership Before Approval

Decision Owner harus jelas sebelum approval diberikan.

⸻

DP-03 — Governance Does Not Transfer Architecture Ownership

Governance review atau governance approval tidak secara otomatis memindahkan architecture ownership.

⸻

DP-04 — Evidence Before Material Conclusion

Material architecture decision harus memiliki evidence yang memadai untuk mendukung conclusion.

⸻

DP-05 — Architecture Baseline Alignment

Architecture decision harus dinilai terhadap applicable Architecture Baseline.

⸻

DP-06 — Explicit Decision State

Setiap material decision harus memiliki state yang jelas.

⸻

DP-07 — Traceability by Design

Decision traceability harus dibangun sejak decision dimulai, bukan ditambahkan setelah decision selesai.

⸻

DP-08 — Accountability Preservation

Delegation tidak menghapus accountability dari delegating authority sesuai applicable governance model.

⸻

DP-09 — Boundary Preservation

Decision governance tidak boleh digunakan untuk menciptakan authority di luar Domain Boundary.

⸻

DP-10 — Review Before Material Commitment

Material decision harus melewati review yang sesuai sebelum menjadi effective.

⸻

7\. Decision Scope

C2 mencakup governance terhadap:

• architecture decision;

• architecture decision proposal;

• architecture decision authority;

• decision ownership;

• decision approval;

• decision evidence;

• decision rationale;

• decision impact;

• decision record;

• decision status;

• decision review;

• decision traceability;

• decision auditability;

• decision implementation status;

• decision closure.

⸻

8\. Out of Scope

C2 tidak menjadi primary owner terhadap:

• Architecture Model;

• Architecture Baseline;

• Architecture Decision domain;

• architecture implementation;

• runtime execution;

• AI engineering execution;

• infrastructure execution;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

• operational decision flow;

• enterprise strategic direction.

Concern tersebut tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

9\. Decision Authority Model

Decision authority mengikuti hierarchy authority yang berlaku.

Canonical model:

Enterprise Authority

↓

Platform Authority

↓

Sub Division Authority

↓

Team Authority

↓

Delegated Authority

Authority hanya dapat digunakan dalam Domain Boundary yang ditetapkan.

Authority level tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengambil keputusan di luar domainnya.

⸻

10\. Architecture Decision Authority

Architecture decision authority tetap berada pada applicable Architecture Authority.

PD-03 tidak memperoleh Architecture Decision Authority hanya karena PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.

Relationship:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

│ Governs

▼

Decision Governance

│

│ applies to

▼

Architecture Decision

│

▼

PD-02

Architecture Authority

Jika architecture decision memiliki governance implication, PD-03 dapat melakukan applicable governance review.

Governance review tidak menggantikan architecture decision.

⸻

11\. Decision Owner

Setiap material architecture decision harus memiliki:

Decision Owner

Decision Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• decision context;

• decision rationale;

• decision completeness;

• impact assessment;

• decision record;

• decision outcome.

Decision Owner harus berasal dari authority domain yang sah.

PD-03 tidak secara otomatis menjadi Decision Owner hanya karena menjalankan governance.

⸻

12\. Decision Sponsor

Jika diperlukan, architecture decision dapat memiliki:

Decision Sponsor

Decision Sponsor memberikan:

• strategic context;

• organizational support;

• escalation support;

• resource context.

Decision Sponsor tidak otomatis menjadi Decision Owner.

⸻

13\. Approving Authority

Approving Authority adalah authority yang memiliki hak memberikan approval terhadap decision sesuai decision class.

Approval harus memiliki:

• authority basis;

• scope;

• decision reference;

• approval state;

• effective status.

Approval authority tidak boleh diasumsikan dari organizational seniority semata.

⸻

14\. Governance Reviewer

Governance Reviewer memastikan architecture decision:

• mengikuti applicable governance;

• memiliki authority basis;

• memiliki required evidence;

• tidak melanggar governance constraint;

• memiliki required record;

• memiliki traceability.

Governance Reviewer tidak otomatis menjadi Architecture Decision Authority.

⸻

15\. Architecture Reviewer

Architecture Reviewer menilai:

• architectural consistency;

• Architecture Baseline alignment;

• architecture impact;

• architectural alternatives;

• architectural consequences;

• domain boundary.

Architecture Reviewer bekerja dalam applicable Architecture Authority.

PD-03 tidak menggantikan Architecture Reviewer.

⸻

16\. Decision Custodian

Decision Custodian bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• decision record;

• metadata;

• version;

• archival state;

• retrieval;

• review history.

Decision Custodian tidak memiliki decision right hanya karena mengelola record.

⸻

17\. Decision Roles

Canonical role separation:

Role Primary Responsibility

Decision Owner Owns decision content and outcome

Decision Sponsor Provides strategic / organizational support

Approving Authority Provides formal approval

Governance Reviewer Validates governance compliance

Architecture Reviewer Validates architecture alignment

Decision Custodian Maintains decision record

Satu individu dapat memegang lebih dari satu role apabila tidak melanggar applicable independence requirement.

⸻

18\. Decision Lifecycle

Architecture decision lifecycle:

Initiated

↓

Drafted

↓

Validated

↓

Impact Assessed

↓

Reviewed

↓

Approved / Rejected

↓

Published

↓

Implemented

↓

Monitored

↓

Reviewed

↓

Closed

↓

Archived

Tidak semua decision harus melalui tingkat lifecycle yang sama.

Decision classification menentukan required governance depth.

⸻

19\. Decision Initiation

Decision dapat dimulai ketika terdapat:

• architecture change proposal;

• architecture conflict;

• new architectural requirement;

• material dependency;

• architectural risk;

• architecture baseline issue;

• domain boundary issue;

• strategic architectural implication.

Initiation tidak berarti decision telah disetujui.

⸻

20\. Decision Intake

Decision intake harus menangkap minimal:

• Decision ID;

• title;

• initiating party;

• decision category;

• decision scope;

• affected domain;

• urgency;

• initial impact;

• required authority;

• related architecture artifact.

Decision intake menjadi entry point untuk governance traceability.

⸻

21\. Decision Classification

Architecture decision harus diklasifikasikan berdasarkan:

• decision domain;

• materiality;

• impact;

• authority level;

• cross-platform effect;

• governance implication;

• implementation consequence.

Classification menentukan:

• reviewer;

• approval path;

• evidence requirement;

• escalation requirement;

• review depth.

⸻

22\. Decision Analysis

Material architecture decision harus mempertimbangkan:

• current state;

• target state;

• problem;

• alternatives;

• constraints;

• dependencies;

• risks;

• benefits;

• consequences;

• reversibility;

• affected boundaries.

Analysis harus dibedakan dari decision.

Analysis

≠

Decision

⸻

23\. Decision Alternatives

Jika terdapat lebih dari satu viable alternative, decision record harus mencatat:

Element Requirement

Alternative Required

Rationale Required

Benefits Required where material

Risks Required where material

Constraints Required

Consequences Required

Reversibility Required where material

Tidak boleh menyembunyikan material alternative hanya untuk mempercepat approval.

⸻

24\. Decision Rationale

Setiap material decision harus memiliki rationale yang menjelaskan:

• why;

• evidence;

• constraints;

• authority basis;

• selected alternative;

• expected consequence.

Rationale harus tetap dapat ditelusuri setelah decision selesai.

⸻

25\. Decision Impact Assessment

Impact assessment harus mempertimbangkan jika relevan:

• architecture;

• governance;

• organization;

• operation;

• security;

• quality;

• runtime;

• AI engineering;

• infrastructure;

• cross-platform dependency.

Impact assessment tidak otomatis memberikan decision right kepada setiap domain yang terdampak.

⸻

26\. Governance Validation

Governance validation memastikan:

1\. authority jelas;

2\. Decision Owner jelas;

3\. required evidence tersedia;

4\. governance requirements dipenuhi;

5\. decision record lengkap;

6\. required review dilakukan;

7\. applicable Architecture Baseline dipertimbangkan;

8\. conflict dan escalation path tersedia.

⸻

27\. Architecture Validation

Architecture validation tetap dilakukan oleh applicable Architecture Authority.

Validation mencakup:

• architectural correctness;

• Architecture Baseline alignment;

• domain boundary;

• dependency;

• architectural consistency;

• architectural consequences.

PD-03 tidak menggantikan architecture validation.

⸻

28\. Approval Model

Canonical flow:

Decision Proposal

↓

Decision Validation

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Governance Review

↓

Architecture Review

↓

Authority Review

↓

Approval / Rejection

↓

Publication

↓

Implementation Authorization

Tidak semua decision membutuhkan seluruh stage dengan kedalaman yang sama.

⸻

29\. Approval Boundary

Approval harus dibedakan dari implementation authorization.

Architecture Approval

↓

Architectural Validity

tidak otomatis berarti:

Implementation Authorization

Implementation tetap membutuhkan authorization dari applicable implementation owner atau authority.

⸻

30\. Rejection

Architecture decision dapat ditolak apabila:

• authority tidak valid;

• evidence tidak memadai;

• architecture impact tidak dapat diterima;

• governance requirement tidak terpenuhi;

• decision berada di luar scope;

• required review belum selesai;

• material conflict belum diselesaikan.

Rejection harus memiliki rationale.

⸻

31\. Conditional Approval

Jika decision dapat diterima dengan condition:

Decision

↓

Condition

↓

Owner

↓

Due / Review State

↓

Verification

Condition harus:

• explicit;

• owned;

• traceable;

• verifiable.

Conditional approval tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan material unresolved issue.

⸻

32\. Decision Publication

Decision yang telah approved harus dipublikasikan melalui canonical decision mechanism.

Publication harus mempertahankan:

• decision ID;

• status;

• effective date;

• authority;

• scope;

• related artifacts;

• conditions;

• implementation state.

⸻

33\. Decision Implementation Boundary

PD-03 governance tidak menjadi implementation executor.

Setelah decision approved:

Architecture Decision

↓

Implementation Owner

↓

Execution

↓

Implementation Evidence

Implementation Owner tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap execution.

⸻

34\. Decision Monitoring

Material architecture decision harus dapat dimonitor apabila:

• implementation belum selesai;

• condition belum closed;

• deviation muncul;

• expected outcome belum tercapai;

• new evidence muncul;

• architecture baseline berubah.

Monitoring tidak mengubah decision secara otomatis.

⸻

35\. Decision Review

Decision review dapat dipicu oleh:

• scheduled review;

• material architecture change;

• new evidence;

• implementation deviation;

• new dependency;

• governance finding;

• strategic change.

Review menghasilkan salah satu:

Maintain

Condition

Amend

Supersede

Revoke

Escalate

Decision review bukan retroactive rewriting.

⸻

36\. Decision History

Historical decision record harus dipertahankan.

Jika decision berubah:

Decision v1

↓

Review

↓

Decision v2

Jangan mengganti historical record v1 seolah-olah v2 selalu berlaku.

Perubahan harus menghasilkan traceable history.

⸻

37\. Decision Register

Decision Register minimal memiliki:

Field Required

Decision ID Yes

Title Yes

Category Yes

Decision Owner Yes

Decision Authority Yes

Governance Reviewer Where applicable

Architecture Reviewer Where applicable

Rationale Yes

Evidence Yes for material decision

Alternatives Where material

Impact Assessment Where material

Approval Record Yes when approval required

Effective Date Yes

Conditions Where applicable

Implementation Status Yes

Review History Yes

Closure State Yes

Archive State Yes

⸻

38\. Decision Traceability

Canonical chain:

Requirement

↓

Decision Trigger

↓

Decision Proposal

↓

Evidence

↓

Analysis

↓

Review

↓

Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Approval

↓

Implementation

↓

Outcome

↓

Review History

Setiap material link harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

39\. Decision Auditability

Decision harus dapat diaudit terhadap:

• who;

• what;

• why;

• when;

• authority;

• evidence;

• approval;

• consequence;

• implementation;

• review.

Auditability tidak berarti seluruh decision harus dipublikasikan kepada seluruh organisasi.

Access tetap mengikuti applicable information governance.

⸻

40\. Decision Exceptions

Decision governance exception harus memiliki:

• exception ID;

• reason;

• scope;

• authority;

• duration;

• compensating control;

• review;

• closure.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent bypass.

⸻

41\. Decision Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

1\. authority tidak jelas;

2\. decision berada di luar scope;

3\. authority conflict terjadi;

4\. cross-domain decision tidak dapat diselesaikan;

5\. strategic implication material;

6\. governance conflict material;

7\. unresolved architecture risk material.

Canonical flow:

Decision Issue

↓

Classify

↓

Applicable Authority Check

↓

Local Resolution

↓

Escalation if Required

↓

Applicable Higher / Cross-Domain Authority

Escalation tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

42\. Cross-Platform Decision

Jika architecture decision berdampak pada lebih dari satu Platform:

Architecture Decision

↓

Affected Platforms

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Cross-Platform Review

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision

Affected Platform tetap mempertahankan domain ownership.

Cross-platform decision tidak berarti PD-03 menjadi owner atas Platform yang terdampak.

⸻

43\. Decision Conflict

Jika terdapat conflict:

Identify Domain

↓

Identify Authority Type

↓

Identify Decision Class

↓

Identify Applicable Precedence

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve / Escalate

Conflict tidak boleh diselesaikan berdasarkan organizational preference semata.

⸻

44\. Architecture Decision vs Governance Decision

Keduanya harus dibedakan.

Decision Type Primary Authority

Architecture Decision Architecture Authority

Governance Decision Governance Authority

Strategic Decision Executive Authority

Operational Decision Applicable Operational Owner

PD-03 mengatur governance mechanism yang memastikan keempatnya berjalan sesuai applicable authority.

⸻

45\. Decision vs Delegation

C2 menetapkan governance terhadap decision.

C3 akan menetapkan delegation governance.

Karena itu C2 hanya menetapkan:

Delegated Authority

↓

Must be traceable

↓

Must remain within scope

Detail delegation lifecycle berada pada C3.

⸻

46\. Decision vs Accountability

Decision Owner tetap accountable terhadap decision sesuai applicable governance model.

Delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus accountability.

Detail accountability governance berada pada C4.

⸻

47\. Decision vs Authority

C2 tidak menggantikan C5.

C5 akan menetapkan detail:

• authority;

• right to act;

• approval;

• veto;

• override;

• delegation;

• authority boundaries.

C2 menggunakan authority model tersebut.

⸻

48\. Decision vs Review

C2 menetapkan bahwa review diperlukan sesuai materiality.

C6 akan menetapkan detailed review governance:

• reviewer;

• review independence;

• finding;

• condition;

• review outcome.

C2 tidak menduplikasi C6.

⸻

49\. Decision Governance Control Model

Decision governance control terdiri dari:

Preventive

↓

Authority Validation

Detective

↓

Decision Review

Corrective

↓

Condition / Remediation

Assurance

↓

Audit / Traceability

Control harus proporsional terhadap decision materiality.

⸻

50\. Decision Materiality

Decision materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• architecture impact;

• number of affected Platforms;

• boundary impact;

• governance impact;

• reversibility;

• security impact;

• operational impact;

• financial/resource implication;

• strategic implication.

Materiality classification harus memiliki basis yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

⸻

51\. Decision Quality Controls

Material architecture decision harus diperiksa terhadap:

DQ-01 — Authority Validity

Apakah decision dibuat dalam authority yang tepat?

DQ-02 — Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup?

DQ-03 — Scope Clarity

Apakah decision scope jelas?

DQ-04 — Alternative Completeness

Apakah material alternatives dipertimbangkan?

DQ-05 — Impact Completeness

Apakah material impacts dinilai?

DQ-06 — Architecture Alignment

Apakah decision aligned dengan Architecture Baseline?

DQ-07 — Governance Compliance

Apakah applicable governance requirement dipenuhi?

DQ-08 — Traceability

Apakah decision dapat ditelusuri?

⸻

52\. Decision Failure States

Decision governance harus dapat menghasilkan state:

Blocked

Rejected

Deferred

Conditioned

Escalated

Superseded

Revoked

Setiap non-success state harus memiliki rationale.

Tidak boleh mengubah status menjadi Approved hanya untuk menghilangkan blocker.

⸻

53\. Governance Independence

Governance review harus memiliki independence yang memadai dari decision subject ketika materiality mengharuskannya.

Reviewer harus mampu menghasilkan conclusion tanpa undue influence dari decision owner.

Independence requirement harus proporsional terhadap:

• materiality;

• conflict of interest;

• authority concentration;

• decision impact.

⸻

54\. Decision Record Integrity

Decision record:

• tidak boleh diubah untuk menghapus historical evidence;

• harus mempertahankan version history;

• harus memiliki attributable author;

• harus mempertahankan approval history;

• harus mempertahankan supersession history.

Correction harus menghasilkan traceable correction record.

⸻

55\. Decision Change

Jika architecture decision harus berubah:

Existing Decision

↓

Change Trigger

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Review

↓

New Decision / Amendment

↓

Approval

↓

Publication

Decision change tidak boleh dilakukan melalui silent overwrite.

⸻

56\. Governance-to-Architecture Interface

Canonical interface:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

├── Governance Requirement

├── Governance Review

├── Compliance Control

└── Decision Governance

│

▼

PD-02 Architecture Authority

│

└── Architecture Decision

PD-03 menjaga governance integrity.

PD-02 menjaga architecture integrity.

Keduanya bekerja melalui defined interface.

⸻

57\. Governance-to-Executive Interface

Jika architecture decision memiliki material strategic implication:

Architecture Decision

↓

Strategic Impact

↓

Executive Review

↓

Applicable Executive Authority

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih Executive Authority.

⸻

58\. Governance-to-Platform Interface

Jika architecture decision berdampak pada Platform lain:

Architecture Decision

↓

Affected Platform

↓

Platform Review

↓

Implementation / Response

Platform owner mempertahankan execution ownership.

⸻

59\. Decision Governance Success Conditions

C2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. setiap material architecture decision memiliki authority basis;

2\. Decision Owner jelas;

3\. decision scope jelas;

4\. required architecture review dilakukan;

5\. governance review dilakukan ketika applicable;

6\. Architecture Baseline alignment dapat dibuktikan;

7\. material decision memiliki evidence;

8\. approval dapat ditelusuri;

9\. implementation authorization dibedakan dari architecture approval;

10\. historical decision record dipertahankan;

11\. decision change memiliki lifecycle;

12\. delegation tidak menghilangkan accountability;

13\. cross-platform impact dapat ditelusuri;

14\. authority conflict memiliki escalation path;

15\. PD-03 tidak mengambil alih Architecture Authority;

16\. architecture decision tetap berada pada applicable Architecture Authority;

17\. decision governance dapat diaudit.

⸻

60\. Canonical Decision Governance Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance governs the integrity, authorization, traceability, accountability, and compliance of architecture decision processes while preserving the decision rights and ownership of the applicable Architecture Authority.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance mengatur integritas, kewenangan, keterlacakan, akuntabilitas, dan kepatuhan proses architecture decision dengan tetap mempertahankan decision rights dan ownership pada Architecture Authority yang berlaku.

⸻

The following rule is normative:

PD-03 governs the decision governance mechanism; it does not acquire Architecture Decision Authority merely by governing that mechanism.

Architecture Authority

Architecture Decision

≠

Implementation Authorization

Decision Governance

≠

Operational Decision Execution

C8 — Cross-Platform Governance
