\# 1. Purpose

C3 menetapkan \*\*Architecture Delegation Governance\*\* bagi PD-03

Governance & Compliance.

C3 mengatur bagaimana authority yang berada dalam Governance &

Compliance domain dapat didelegasikan secara:

\- sah;

\- eksplisit;

\- terbatas;

\- terdokumentasi;

\- dapat ditinjau;

\- dapat dicabut;

\- dapat ditelusuri;

\- dapat diaudit.

C3 memastikan bahwa delegation:

\`\`\`text

Authority

↓

Delegation

↓

Defined Scope

↓

Controlled Exercise

↓

Review

↓

Revocation / Renewal

Delegation bukan:

• transfer organizational ownership;

• creation of new constitutional authority;

• unrestricted authority transfer;

• automatic accountability transfer;

• mechanism untuk mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

⸻

2\. Delegation Constitution

Delegation Constitution merupakan constitutional mechanism yang mengatur bagaimana PD-03 memberikan hak untuk menjalankan authority tertentu kepada pihak lain dalam scope yang sah.

Setiap delegation harus:

• berasal dari Authority Owner yang sah;

• memiliki delegated authority yang jelas;

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki constraints;

• memiliki effective state;

• memiliki duration atau termination condition;

• memiliki record;

• dapat direview;

• dapat dicabut;

• mempertahankan ownership;

• mempertahankan accountability sesuai applicable authority model.

Tidak diperbolehkan terdapat informal delegation yang memberikan material governance right tanpa authority basis.

⸻

3\. Governance Position of Delegation

PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

Delegation digunakan untuk memungkinkan governance responsibility dijalankan secara efektif tanpa mengubah constitutional boundary.

Model:

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Delegation Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Authority

↓

Governance Action

Delegated authority tetap berada dalam boundary yang berasal dari delegating authority.

⸻

4\. Architecture Delegation Boundary

Istilah Architecture Delegation Governance pada C3 berarti governance terhadap delegation yang berhubungan dengan architecture decision atau architecture-related governance.

C3 tidak memberikan Architecture Authority kepada PD-03.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

└── Governs Delegation

│

▼

Governance Rights

Sedangkan:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

│

└── Owns Architecture Decision Authority

Dengan demikian:

PD-03 Delegation Governance

≠

PD-03 Architecture Authority

Delegation yang menyangkut Architecture Authority harus tetap berasal dari applicable Architecture Authority.

⸻

5\. Delegation Objectives

DG-01 — Controlled Delegation

Memastikan seluruh delegation dilakukan melalui mechanism yang terkendali.

⸻

DG-02 — Authority Continuity

Memungkinkan governance function tetap berjalan ketika Authority Owner tidak melakukan seluruh action secara langsung.

⸻

DG-03 — Explicit Delegation

Memastikan setiap delegation dapat diidentifikasi secara eksplisit.

⸻

DG-04 — Scope Integrity

Memastikan delegated authority tidak melebihi delegated scope.

⸻

DG-05 — Ownership Preservation

Memastikan delegation tidak memindahkan organizational ownership.

⸻

DG-06 — Accountability Preservation

Memastikan delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus final accountability dari delegating authority.

⸻

DG-07 — Auditability

Memastikan delegation dapat diverifikasi dan diaudit.

⸻

DG-08 — Revocability

Memastikan delegation dapat dihentikan ketika tidak lagi valid.

⸻

6\. Delegation Principles

DP-01 — Authority Before Delegation

Hanya authority owner yang memiliki right to delegate yang dapat menerbitkan delegation.

⸻

DP-02 — Explicit Delegation

Delegation harus dinyatakan secara eksplisit.

Informal expectation tidak dianggap sebagai formal delegation.

⸻

DP-03 — Scope Before Exercise

Delegated authority tidak boleh digunakan sebelum scope-nya jelas.

⸻

DP-04 — Least Necessary Authority

Delegation hanya memberikan authority yang diperlukan untuk menjalankan tujuan delegation.

⸻

DP-05 — Ownership Retention

Delegation tidak mengubah ownership domain.

⸻

DP-06 — Accountability Retention

Delegation tidak secara otomatis memindahkan final accountability.

⸻

DP-07 — Time / Condition Bound

Delegation harus memiliki:

• expiry date; atau

• review date; atau

• termination condition.

⸻

DP-08 — Revocability

Delegation harus dapat dicabut oleh authority yang berwenang.

⸻

DP-09 — Traceability by Design

Delegation harus dapat ditelusuri dari issuance sampai termination.

⸻

DP-10 — No Authority Inflation

Delegated authority tidak boleh menghasilkan authority yang lebih besar daripada authority yang dapat didelegasikan.

⸻

7\. Delegation Scope

Delegation harus mendefinisikan minimal:

• delegated domain;

• delegated action;

• delegated decision class;

• delegated approval class jika applicable;

• permitted activity;

• prohibited activity;

• constraints;

• effective period;

• termination condition.

Canonical rule:

Delegated Authority

≤

Delegable Authority

≤

Authority Owner's Valid Authority

⸻

8\. Delegable Authority

Tidak semua authority dapat didelegasikan.

Authority owner harus mengidentifikasi:

1\. authority yang dapat didelegasikan;

2\. authority yang hanya dapat digunakan oleh authority owner;

3\. authority yang membutuhkan higher approval;

4\. authority yang membutuhkan independent review;

5\. authority yang tidak dapat didelegasikan.

⸻

9\. Non-Delegable Authority

Authority berikut tidak boleh dianggap delegated hanya karena terdapat operational need:

• constitutional authority;

• enterprise ownership;

• Platform ownership;

• authority yang secara eksplisit reserved;

• authority yang berada pada domain authority lain;

• authority yang membutuhkan direct exercise berdasarkan applicable governance rule.

Jika delegation eligibility tidak jelas:

No Assumption

↓

Authority Review

↓

Decision

⸻

10\. Delegation Authority Model

Delegation mengikuti authority hierarchy yang berlaku.

Authority Level Delegation Boundary

Executive Authority Hanya authority yang secara eksplisit dapat didelegasikan

Platform Authority Authority dalam Platform domain yang dapat didelegasikan

Sub-Division Authority Authority dalam Sub-Division scope

Team Authority Authority dalam Team scope

Delegated Authority Tidak boleh re-delegate kecuali secara eksplisit diizinkan

Delegation tidak boleh melampaui:

• Domain Boundary;

• Authority Level;

• applicable decision class;

• applicable approval boundary.

⸻

11\. PD-03 Delegation Authority

PD-03 dapat mendelegasikan governance authority apabila delegation dibutuhkan untuk menjalankan:

• policy governance;

• standards governance;

• approval governance;

• certification governance;

• compliance governance;

• governance review;

• governance administration;

• governance evidence management.

Delegation tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance domain.

⸻

12\. Architecture-Related Delegation

Jika architecture matter membutuhkan delegated governance action:

Architecture Matter

↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Delegation

Jika governance matter memiliki architecture implication:

Governance Matter

↓

PD-03 Governance Authority

↓

Governance Delegation

Kedua jalur tidak boleh dicampur.

Architecture implication tidak otomatis memberikan Architecture Authority kepada PD-03.

⸻

13\. Delegation Roles

Delegation governance menggunakan role berikut.

Role Responsibility

Delegating Authority Memberikan delegation

Delegated Authority Menjalankan delegated authority

Domain Owner Mempertahankan domain ownership

Governance Reviewer Memastikan delegation sesuai governance

Compliance Reviewer Memastikan compliance terhadap delegation rules

Delegation Custodian Memelihara delegation record

Reviewing Authority Menilai continuation / renewal / revocation

Satu individu dapat menjalankan lebih dari satu role apabila tidak menimbulkan conflict atau melanggar independence requirement.

⸻

14\. Delegating Authority

Delegating Authority harus:

• memiliki authority yang sah;

• memiliki right to delegate;

• mengetahui delegated scope;

• menetapkan constraints;

• menetapkan effective period;

• memastikan record tersedia;

• melakukan review apabila diperlukan.

Delegating Authority tidak boleh memberikan delegation yang melampaui authority-nya sendiri.

⸻

15\. Delegated Authority

Delegated Authority harus:

• hanya menggunakan authority dalam scope;

• mematuhi constraints;

• menjaga evidence;

• menjaga decision record;

• melaporkan material issue;

• tidak memperluas authority secara implisit;

• tidak melakukan re-delegation tanpa explicit permission.

⸻

16\. Delegation Record

Setiap material delegation harus memiliki:

Field Requirement

Delegation ID Required

Title Required

Delegating Authority Required

Delegated Authority Required

Authority Type Required

Domain Required

Decision Class Where applicable

Approval Class Where applicable

Scope Required

Constraints Required

Effective Date Required

Expiry / Review Date Required

Termination Condition Required

Related Policy Where applicable

Related Decision Where applicable

Related Architecture Matter Where applicable

Review History Required

Revocation Status Required

Audit Record Required

⸻

17\. Delegation Register

PD-03 harus mempertahankan Delegation Register sebagai canonical record untuk material delegation.

Delegation Register harus memungkinkan identification:

Who delegated?

↓

What was delegated?

↓

To whom?

↓

Within what scope?

↓

For how long?

↓

Under what conditions?

↓

Was it reviewed?

↓

Was it revoked / renewed?

⸻

18\. Delegation Lifecycle

Lifecycle canonical:

Delegation Request

↓

Eligibility Assessment

↓

Authority Validation

↓

Scope Definition

↓

Risk / Impact Assessment

↓

Delegation Approval

↓

Activation

↓

Monitoring

↓

Review

↓

Renewal / Modification

↓

Revocation / Expiry

↓

Archive

Tidak semua delegation memerlukan tingkat assessment yang sama.

Materiality menentukan review depth.

⸻

19\. Delegation Request

Delegation request harus menjelaskan:

• reason;

• intended outcome;

• proposed delegated authority;

• proposed recipient;

• scope;

• duration;

• constraints;

• material dependencies;

• potential conflicts.

Request bukan delegation.

Delegation hanya effective setelah approval yang diperlukan.

⸻

20\. Eligibility Assessment

Sebelum delegation diberikan, authority owner harus menentukan:

1\. apakah authority dapat didelegasikan;

2\. apakah recipient memiliki eligibility;

3\. apakah scope dapat dibatasi;

4\. apakah delegation menimbulkan conflict;

5\. apakah delegation membutuhkan additional approval;

6\. apakah delegation memerlukan independent review.

Jika salah satu material condition tidak terpenuhi:

Delegation

↓

BLOCKED

⸻

21\. Authority Validation

Authority validation memastikan:

• delegating authority valid;

• delegated authority valid;

• authority type valid;

• scope valid;

• decision class valid;

• duration valid;

• applicable constraints valid.

Delegation tanpa authority basis tidak sah.

⸻

22\. Scope Definition

Scope delegation harus menjawab:

• What?

• Where?

• When?

• Under what condition?

• With what limitation?

• What is excluded?

Canonical structure:

Delegated Authority

│

├── Allowed

├── Restricted

└── Prohibited

⸻

23\. Delegation Constraints

Delegation dapat dibatasi berdasarkan:

• decision class;

• monetary/resource threshold jika applicable;

• geographic/domain boundary;

• Platform boundary;

• time;

• condition;

• evidence requirement;

• review requirement;

• approval requirement.

Constraint harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

24\. Delegation Activation

Delegation menjadi effective hanya setelah:

1\. authority validation;

2\. required approval;

3\. formal record;

4\. effective date reached.

Sebelum activation:

Delegation Status = NOT ACTIVE

⸻

25\. Delegation Monitoring

Material delegation harus dimonitor terhadap:

• usage;

• scope compliance;

• decision quality;

• evidence completeness;

• exceptions;

• conflicts;

• expiry;

• conditions.

Monitoring tidak berarti setiap delegated action harus disetujui ulang.

⸻

26\. Delegation Review

Review diperlukan apabila:

• review date tercapai;

• material scope change terjadi;

• authority structure berubah;

• organizational boundary berubah;

• conflict muncul;

• repeated exception terjadi;

• material governance finding muncul;

• delegation tidak lagi diperlukan.

Review menghasilkan:

Continue

Modify

Suspend

Renew

Revoke

Escalate

⸻

27\. Delegation Renewal

Renewal bukan automatic continuation.

Renewal harus mempertimbangkan:

• original purpose;

• current need;

• authority validity;

• performance of delegated authority;

• findings;

• scope;

• organizational change.

Renewal harus menghasilkan updated record jika terdapat perubahan.

⸻

28\. Delegation Modification

Jika scope berubah:

Existing Delegation

↓

Change Request

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Authority Validation

↓

Approval

↓

Updated Delegation

Scope tidak boleh diperluas melalui informal agreement.

⸻

29\. Delegation Revocation

Delegation dapat dicabut apabila:

• expiry tercapai;

• purpose selesai;

• authority owner mencabut;

• recipient tidak lagi eligible;

• scope dilanggar;

• governance violation terjadi;

• organizational boundary berubah;

• authority model berubah;

• material conflict muncul.

Revocation harus memiliki:

• reason;

• effective date;

• authority;

• affected scope;

• record reference.

⸻

30\. Delegation Suspension

Jika terdapat immediate concern tetapi revocation belum final:

Active

↓

Suspended

↓

Review

├── Resume

├── Modify

└── Revoke

Suspension harus memiliki authority basis.

⸻

31\. Delegation Expiry

Delegation yang mencapai expiry tidak boleh digunakan lagi kecuali renewal telah effective.

Expired

≠

Active

Continuation setelah expiry harus diperlakukan sebagai new or renewed delegation sesuai applicable mechanism.

⸻

32\. Re-Delegation

Delegated Authority tidak boleh melakukan re-delegation secara default.

Re-delegation hanya diperbolehkan jika:

• explicitly authorized;

• scope memungkinkan;

• record dapat ditelusuri;

• accountability chain tetap jelas.

Canonical chain:

Authority Owner

↓

Delegated Authority

↓

Sub-Delegated Authority

Setiap additional level meningkatkan governance risk dan karena itu harus memiliki explicit basis.

⸻

33\. Delegation and Ownership

Delegation tidak mengubah ownership.

Ownership

│

└── Remains with Domain Owner

Authority

│

└── May be Delegated

Untuk PD-03:

Governance Domain

↓

PD-03 Ownership / Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Action

Delegated actor tidak menjadi owner atas Governance & Compliance domain hanya karena menerima delegation.

⸻

34\. Delegation and Accountability

Delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus accountability.

Delegating Authority

↓

Final Accountability

sedangkan:

Delegated Authority

↓

Execution of Delegated Right

Detailed accountability relationship berada pada C4.

C3 hanya menetapkan preservation principle.

⸻

35\. Delegation and Decision Governance

C2 menetapkan decision governance.

C3 mengatur bagaimana authority untuk menjalankan decision-related action dapat didelegasikan.

Relationship:

C2

Decision Governance

↓

Decision Authority

↓

C3

Delegation Governance

↓

Delegated Decision Right

Delegation tidak mengubah decision governance requirements.

⸻

36\. Delegation and Authority Governance

C5 menjadi authoritative section untuk detailed authority rights.

C3 menggunakan authority model tersebut.

Relationship:

C5

Authority Governance

↓

What authority exists?

↓

C3

Delegation Governance

↓

Which authority may be delegated?

C3 tidak menciptakan authority category baru.

⸻

37\. Delegation and Accountability Governance

C4 menentukan detailed accountability.

C3 memastikan delegation mempertahankan accountability chain.

Delegation

↓

Accountability Preservation

↓

C4

C3 tidak menggantikan accountability matrix.

⸻

38\. Delegation and Review Governance

C6 menentukan detailed review mechanism.

C3 menyediakan object yang dapat direview:

• delegation validity;

• scope;

• usage;

• compliance;

• effectiveness;

• renewal;

• revocation.

Review conclusion harus mengikuti C6.

⸻

39\. Delegation and Escalation Governance

Jika delegated authority tidak mencukupi untuk menyelesaikan matter:

Delegated Authority

↓

Authority Boundary Reached

↓

Escalation

↓

Applicable Authority

Delegated actor tidak boleh memperluas authority sendiri untuk menghindari escalation.

C7 menjadi authoritative mechanism untuk escalation.

⸻

40\. Cross-Platform Delegation

Delegation yang berdampak pada Platform lain harus menghormati:

• Platform ownership;

• Domain Boundary;

• applicable authority;

• architecture authority;

• governance authority.

PD-03 tidak boleh mendelegasikan kepada pihak lain sebuah authority yang sebenarnya dimiliki oleh Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-03 Governance Authority

↓

Governance Requirement

↓

Delegated Governance Action

NOT:

PD-03

↓

Delegated Architecture Authority

↓

PD-02 Architecture Domain

⸻

41\. Architecture Decision Delegation

Untuk architecture decision, delegation harus mengikuti applicable Architecture Authority.

Jika PD-02 mendelegasikan architecture decision authority:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Delegation

↓

Delegated Architecture Authority

PD-03 hanya mengatur governance requirement yang berlaku terhadap delegation tersebut.

PD-03 tidak menjadi source of Architecture Authority.

⸻

42\. Governance Decision Delegation

Untuk governance decision:

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Governance Decision Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Authority

Delegation harus tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance domain.

⸻

43\. Policy Delegation

Policy-related authority dapat didelegasikan jika applicable authority model mengizinkannya.

Delegation harus menetapkan:

• policy scope;

• permitted action;

• approval requirement;

• review requirement;

• expiry;

• record.

Delegation tidak membuat delegated actor menjadi policy owner secara otomatis.

⸻

44\. Standards Delegation

Standards-related action dapat didelegasikan dalam scope yang ditetapkan.

Contoh action:

• drafting;

• maintenance;

• review coordination;

• evidence collection;

• implementation monitoring.

Final authority terhadap standards tetap mengikuti applicable authority model.

⸻

45\. Approval Delegation

Approval authority hanya dapat didelegasikan apabila authority model mengizinkannya.

Delegation harus menetapkan:

• approval class;

• scope;

• threshold jika applicable;

• constraints;

• effective period.

Delegated approval harus tetap dapat ditelusuri kepada delegating authority.

⸻

46\. Certification Delegation

Certification-related activity dapat didelegasikan jika permitted.

Delegation harus membedakan:

Assessment

≠

Certification Decision

Pendelegasian assessment activity tidak otomatis memberikan certification authority.

⸻

47\. Compliance Delegation

Compliance activity dapat didelegasikan untuk:

• evidence collection;

• assessment;

• monitoring;

• corrective-action follow-up;

• verification.

Namun delegated compliance activity harus tetap berada dalam applicable governance framework.

⸻

48\. Delegation Materiality

Delegation materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• authority level;

• decision impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• reversibility;

• duration;

• strategic implication;

• compliance implication;

• architecture implication;

• potential conflict of interest.

Material delegation membutuhkan review yang lebih ketat.

⸻

49\. Delegation Risk

Risiko delegation dapat meliputi:

• authority overreach;

• scope ambiguity;

• accountability gap;

• conflict of interest;

• unauthorized re-delegation;

• stale delegation;

• expired delegation;

• inadequate evidence;

• cross-platform conflict.

Risk harus dikelola melalui:

• scope;

• constraints;

• monitoring;

• review;

• revocation;

• escalation.

⸻

50\. Delegation Independence

Jika delegated authority melakukan review atau assessment terhadap matter yang memiliki conflict of interest, applicable review independence requirement harus diterapkan.

Delegation tidak boleh digunakan untuk menghindari independence.

Delegation

≠

Independence Waiver

⸻

51\. Delegation Evidence

Material delegation harus memiliki evidence:

• authority basis;

• approval;

• scope;

• effective status;

• usage;

• review;

• exceptions;

• revocation / renewal.

Evidence menjadi bagian dari Delegation Register atau linked governance record.

⸻

52\. Delegation Auditability

Delegation harus dapat diaudit terhadap:

• who delegated;

• who received;

• what was delegated;

• authority basis;

• scope;

• duration;

• conditions;

• actions taken;

• review;

• renewal;

• revocation.

Auditability harus tersedia tanpa mengubah historical evidence.

⸻

53\. Historical Delegation Integrity

Historical delegation record tidak boleh diubah untuk menghapus:

• original scope;

• original authority;

• original approval;

• historical action;

• revocation history.

Jika correction diperlukan:

Original Record

↓

Correction Record

↓

Traceable History

⸻

54\. Delegation Exception

Exception terhadap delegation rule harus memiliki:

• exception ID;

• reason;

• scope;

• authority;

• duration;

• compensating control;

• review;

• closure.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent informal delegation.

⸻

55\. Delegation Failure States

Delegation governance harus mendukung state:

Draft

Pending

Approved

Active

Suspended

Modified

Expired

Revoked

Rejected

Escalated

Archived

Status harus merefleksikan actual governance state.

⸻

56\. Unauthorized Delegation

Jika delegation ditemukan tanpa authority basis:

Unauthorized Delegation

↓

Record Finding

↓

Suspend / Stop Exercise

↓

Authority Review

↓

Correct / Revoke

↓

Escalate if Material

Unauthorized delegation tidak boleh dianggap valid hanya karena telah digunakan sebelumnya.

⸻

57\. Delegation Scope Violation

Jika delegated authority melampaui scope:

Scope Violation

↓

Record

↓

Assess Impact

↓

Suspend if Required

↓

Correct

↓

Review

↓

Escalate if Material

Delegated actor tidak boleh memperluas scope secara unilateral.

⸻

58\. Delegation Register Governance

Delegation Register harus memiliki:

• unique identifier;

• current status;

• authority owner;

• delegated party;

• scope;

• effective period;

• related records;

• review status;

• revocation status.

Register harus memiliki single authoritative source.

⸻

59\. Delegation Change Control

Perubahan terhadap delegation mechanism harus mengikuti governance change control.

Material changes harus mempertimbangkan:

• authority impact;

• organizational impact;

• accountability impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• architecture implication;

• compliance implication.

Change tidak boleh dilakukan melalui silent overwrite.

⸻

60\. Delegation Governance Control Model

Delegation control menggunakan:

Preventive Control

↓

Authority / Eligibility Validation

Detective Control

↓

Delegation Review / Monitoring

Corrective Control

↓

Modification / Suspension / Revocation

Assurance Control

↓

Audit / Traceability

Control depth harus proporsional terhadap delegation materiality.

⸻

61\. Governance Interface

PD-03 delegation governance berinteraksi dengan:

Executive Authority

│

▼

PD-03 Governance Authority

│

├── Policy

├── Standards

├── Approval

├── Certification

└── Compliance

│

▼

Delegated Authority

Interface tersebut tidak menciptakan organizational hierarchy baru.

⸻

62\. Architecture Interface

Relationship dengan PD-02:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

│

│ Architecture Delegation

▼

Delegated Architecture Authority

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

│ Governance of delegation

▼

Governance Compliance

PD-03 dapat menilai governance compliance atas delegation.

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner delegated architecture authority.

⸻

63\. Cross-Platform Boundary

Delegation tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengubah:

• Platform ownership;

• Domain ownership;

• Architecture Authority;

• Security Authority;

• Quality Authority;

• operational ownership.

Jika cross-platform delegation diperlukan:

1\. identify originating authority;

2\. identify delegated scope;

3\. identify affected Platform;

4\. validate authority;

5\. validate boundary;

6\. record delegation;

7\. review material impact.

⸻

64\. Delegation Success Conditions

C3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. setiap delegation memiliki authority basis;

2\. delegating authority memiliki right to delegate;

3\. delegated authority teridentifikasi;

4\. scope eksplisit;

5\. constraints eksplisit;

6\. duration / termination condition tersedia;

7\. ownership tetap terjaga;

8\. accountability tetap dapat ditelusuri;

9\. delegation dapat direview;

10\. delegation dapat dicabut;

11\. Delegation Register tersedia;

12\. material delegation dapat diaudit;

13\. unauthorized delegation dapat dideteksi;

14\. scope violation memiliki handling;

15\. re-delegation dikendalikan;

16\. cross-platform delegation menjaga domain boundary;

17\. architecture delegation tetap berada pada Architecture Authority yang applicable;

18\. PD-03 tidak menciptakan Architecture Authority;

19\. delegation mechanism dapat diwariskan ke governance domain lain tanpa mengubah constitutional boundary.

⸻

65\. Canonical Delegation Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance governs the delegation of authorized governance rights through explicit scope, authority validation, lifecycle control, accountability preservation, review, traceability, and revocability, while preserving organizational ownership and the authority boundaries of Architecture and other Platform Domains.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance mengatur pendelegasian governance rights yang sah melalui scope yang eksplisit, validasi authority, lifecycle control, preservasi accountability, review, traceability, dan revocability, dengan tetap menjaga organizational ownership serta boundary authority Architecture dan Platform Domain lainnya.

⸻

68\. Canonical Boundary Rule

The following rule is normative:

Delegation grants a bounded right to act; it does not create ownership, constitutional authority, or authority beyond the delegating authority's valid scope.

Therefore:

Delegation

≠

Ownership Transfer

Delegation

≠

Unlimited Authority

Delegation

≠

Automatic Accountability Transfer

Delegation

≠

New Constitutional Authority

Governance Delegation

≠

Architecture Authority

C8 — Cross-Platform Architecture Governance

C3

Delegation

├── C7 → What happens when delegation is insufficient?

C3 tidak mengambil alih concern tersebut.

\# 1. Purpose

C3 menetapkan \*\*Architecture Delegation Governance\*\* bagi PD-03

Governance & Compliance.

C3 mengatur bagaimana authority yang berada dalam Governance &

Compliance domain dapat didelegasikan secara:

\- sah;

\- eksplisit;

\- terbatas;

\- terdokumentasi;

\- dapat ditinjau;

\- dapat dicabut;

\- dapat ditelusuri;

\- dapat diaudit.

C3 memastikan bahwa delegation:

\`\`\`text

Authority

↓

Delegation

↓

Defined Scope

↓

Controlled Exercise

↓

Review

↓

Revocation / Renewal

Delegation bukan:

• transfer organizational ownership;

• creation of new constitutional authority;

• unrestricted authority transfer;

• automatic accountability transfer;

• mechanism untuk mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

⸻

2\. Delegation Constitution

Delegation Constitution merupakan constitutional mechanism yang mengatur bagaimana PD-03 memberikan hak untuk menjalankan authority tertentu kepada pihak lain dalam scope yang sah.

Setiap delegation harus:

• berasal dari Authority Owner yang sah;

• memiliki delegated authority yang jelas;

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki constraints;

• memiliki effective state;

• memiliki duration atau termination condition;

• memiliki record;

• dapat direview;

• dapat dicabut;

• mempertahankan ownership;

• mempertahankan accountability sesuai applicable authority model.

Tidak diperbolehkan terdapat informal delegation yang memberikan material governance right tanpa authority basis.

⸻

3\. Governance Position of Delegation

PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

Delegation digunakan untuk memungkinkan governance responsibility dijalankan secara efektif tanpa mengubah constitutional boundary.

Model:

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Delegation Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Authority

↓

Governance Action

Delegated authority tetap berada dalam boundary yang berasal dari delegating authority.

⸻

4\. Architecture Delegation Boundary

Istilah Architecture Delegation Governance pada C3 berarti governance terhadap delegation yang berhubungan dengan architecture decision atau architecture-related governance.

C3 tidak memberikan Architecture Authority kepada PD-03.

Canonical boundary:

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

└── Governs Delegation

│

▼

Governance Rights

Sedangkan:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

│

└── Owns Architecture Decision Authority

Dengan demikian:

PD-03 Delegation Governance

≠

PD-03 Architecture Authority

Delegation yang menyangkut Architecture Authority harus tetap berasal dari applicable Architecture Authority.

⸻

5\. Delegation Objectives

DG-01 — Controlled Delegation

Memastikan seluruh delegation dilakukan melalui mechanism yang terkendali.

⸻

DG-02 — Authority Continuity

Memungkinkan governance function tetap berjalan ketika Authority Owner tidak melakukan seluruh action secara langsung.

⸻

DG-03 — Explicit Delegation

Memastikan setiap delegation dapat diidentifikasi secara eksplisit.

⸻

DG-04 — Scope Integrity

Memastikan delegated authority tidak melebihi delegated scope.

⸻

DG-05 — Ownership Preservation

Memastikan delegation tidak memindahkan organizational ownership.

⸻

DG-06 — Accountability Preservation

Memastikan delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus final accountability dari delegating authority.

⸻

DG-07 — Auditability

Memastikan delegation dapat diverifikasi dan diaudit.

⸻

DG-08 — Revocability

Memastikan delegation dapat dihentikan ketika tidak lagi valid.

⸻

6\. Delegation Principles

DP-01 — Authority Before Delegation

Hanya authority owner yang memiliki right to delegate yang dapat menerbitkan delegation.

⸻

DP-02 — Explicit Delegation

Delegation harus dinyatakan secara eksplisit.

Informal expectation tidak dianggap sebagai formal delegation.

⸻

DP-03 — Scope Before Exercise

Delegated authority tidak boleh digunakan sebelum scope-nya jelas.

⸻

DP-04 — Least Necessary Authority

Delegation hanya memberikan authority yang diperlukan untuk menjalankan tujuan delegation.

⸻

DP-05 — Ownership Retention

Delegation tidak mengubah ownership domain.

⸻

DP-06 — Accountability Retention

Delegation tidak secara otomatis memindahkan final accountability.

⸻

DP-07 — Time / Condition Bound

Delegation harus memiliki:

• expiry date; atau

• review date; atau

• termination condition.

⸻

DP-08 — Revocability

Delegation harus dapat dicabut oleh authority yang berwenang.

⸻

DP-09 — Traceability by Design

Delegation harus dapat ditelusuri dari issuance sampai termination.

⸻

DP-10 — No Authority Inflation

Delegated authority tidak boleh menghasilkan authority yang lebih besar daripada authority yang dapat didelegasikan.

⸻

7\. Delegation Scope

Delegation harus mendefinisikan minimal:

• delegated domain;

• delegated action;

• delegated decision class;

• delegated approval class jika applicable;

• permitted activity;

• prohibited activity;

• constraints;

• effective period;

• termination condition.

Canonical rule:

Delegated Authority

≤

Delegable Authority

≤

Authority Owner's Valid Authority

⸻

8\. Delegable Authority

Tidak semua authority dapat didelegasikan.

Authority owner harus mengidentifikasi:

1\. authority yang dapat didelegasikan;

2\. authority yang hanya dapat digunakan oleh authority owner;

3\. authority yang membutuhkan higher approval;

4\. authority yang membutuhkan independent review;

5\. authority yang tidak dapat didelegasikan.

⸻

9\. Non-Delegable Authority

Authority berikut tidak boleh dianggap delegated hanya karena terdapat operational need:

• constitutional authority;

• enterprise ownership;

• Platform ownership;

• authority yang secara eksplisit reserved;

• authority yang berada pada domain authority lain;

• authority yang membutuhkan direct exercise berdasarkan applicable governance rule.

Jika delegation eligibility tidak jelas:

No Assumption

↓

Authority Review

↓

Decision

⸻

10\. Delegation Authority Model

Delegation mengikuti authority hierarchy yang berlaku.

Authority Level Delegation Boundary

Executive Authority Hanya authority yang secara eksplisit dapat didelegasikan

Platform Authority Authority dalam Platform domain yang dapat didelegasikan

Sub-Division Authority Authority dalam Sub-Division scope

Team Authority Authority dalam Team scope

Delegated Authority Tidak boleh re-delegate kecuali secara eksplisit diizinkan

Delegation tidak boleh melampaui:

• Domain Boundary;

• Authority Level;

• applicable decision class;

• applicable approval boundary.

⸻

11\. PD-03 Delegation Authority

PD-03 dapat mendelegasikan governance authority apabila delegation dibutuhkan untuk menjalankan:

• policy governance;

• standards governance;

• approval governance;

• certification governance;

• compliance governance;

• governance review;

• governance administration;

• governance evidence management.

Delegation tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance domain.

⸻

12\. Architecture-Related Delegation

Jika architecture matter membutuhkan delegated governance action:

Architecture Matter

↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Delegation

Jika governance matter memiliki architecture implication:

Governance Matter

↓

PD-03 Governance Authority

↓

Governance Delegation

Kedua jalur tidak boleh dicampur.

Architecture implication tidak otomatis memberikan Architecture Authority kepada PD-03.

⸻

13\. Delegation Roles

Delegation governance menggunakan role berikut.

Role Responsibility

Delegating Authority Memberikan delegation

Delegated Authority Menjalankan delegated authority

Domain Owner Mempertahankan domain ownership

Governance Reviewer Memastikan delegation sesuai governance

Compliance Reviewer Memastikan compliance terhadap delegation rules

Delegation Custodian Memelihara delegation record

Reviewing Authority Menilai continuation / renewal / revocation

Satu individu dapat menjalankan lebih dari satu role apabila tidak menimbulkan conflict atau melanggar independence requirement.

⸻

14\. Delegating Authority

Delegating Authority harus:

• memiliki authority yang sah;

• memiliki right to delegate;

• mengetahui delegated scope;

• menetapkan constraints;

• menetapkan effective period;

• memastikan record tersedia;

• melakukan review apabila diperlukan.

Delegating Authority tidak boleh memberikan delegation yang melampaui authority-nya sendiri.

⸻

15\. Delegated Authority

Delegated Authority harus:

• hanya menggunakan authority dalam scope;

• mematuhi constraints;

• menjaga evidence;

• menjaga decision record;

• melaporkan material issue;

• tidak memperluas authority secara implisit;

• tidak melakukan re-delegation tanpa explicit permission.

⸻

16\. Delegation Record

Setiap material delegation harus memiliki:

Field Requirement

Delegation ID Required

Title Required

Delegating Authority Required

Delegated Authority Required

Authority Type Required

Domain Required

Decision Class Where applicable

Approval Class Where applicable

Scope Required

Constraints Required

Effective Date Required

Expiry / Review Date Required

Termination Condition Required

Related Policy Where applicable

Related Decision Where applicable

Related Architecture Matter Where applicable

Review History Required

Revocation Status Required

Audit Record Required

⸻

17\. Delegation Register

PD-03 harus mempertahankan Delegation Register sebagai canonical record untuk material delegation.

Delegation Register harus memungkinkan identification:

Who delegated?

↓

What was delegated?

↓

To whom?

↓

Within what scope?

↓

For how long?

↓

Under what conditions?

↓

Was it reviewed?

↓

Was it revoked / renewed?

⸻

18\. Delegation Lifecycle

Lifecycle canonical:

Delegation Request

↓

Eligibility Assessment

↓

Authority Validation

↓

Scope Definition

↓

Risk / Impact Assessment

↓

Delegation Approval

↓

Activation

↓

Monitoring

↓

Review

↓

Renewal / Modification

↓

Revocation / Expiry

↓

Archive

Tidak semua delegation memerlukan tingkat assessment yang sama.

Materiality menentukan review depth.

⸻

19\. Delegation Request

Delegation request harus menjelaskan:

• reason;

• intended outcome;

• proposed delegated authority;

• proposed recipient;

• scope;

• duration;

• constraints;

• material dependencies;

• potential conflicts.

Request bukan delegation.

Delegation hanya effective setelah approval yang diperlukan.

⸻

20\. Eligibility Assessment

Sebelum delegation diberikan, authority owner harus menentukan:

1\. apakah authority dapat didelegasikan;

2\. apakah recipient memiliki eligibility;

3\. apakah scope dapat dibatasi;

4\. apakah delegation menimbulkan conflict;

5\. apakah delegation membutuhkan additional approval;

6\. apakah delegation memerlukan independent review.

Jika salah satu material condition tidak terpenuhi:

Delegation

↓

BLOCKED

⸻

21\. Authority Validation

Authority validation memastikan:

• delegating authority valid;

• delegated authority valid;

• authority type valid;

• scope valid;

• decision class valid;

• duration valid;

• applicable constraints valid.

Delegation tanpa authority basis tidak sah.

⸻

22\. Scope Definition

Scope delegation harus menjawab:

• What?

• Where?

• When?

• Under what condition?

• With what limitation?

• What is excluded?

Canonical structure:

Delegated Authority

│

├── Allowed

├── Restricted

└── Prohibited

⸻

23\. Delegation Constraints

Delegation dapat dibatasi berdasarkan:

• decision class;

• monetary/resource threshold jika applicable;

• geographic/domain boundary;

• Platform boundary;

• time;

• condition;

• evidence requirement;

• review requirement;

• approval requirement.

Constraint harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

24\. Delegation Activation

Delegation menjadi effective hanya setelah:

1\. authority validation;

2\. required approval;

3\. formal record;

4\. effective date reached.

Sebelum activation:

Delegation Status = NOT ACTIVE

⸻

25\. Delegation Monitoring

Material delegation harus dimonitor terhadap:

• usage;

• scope compliance;

• decision quality;

• evidence completeness;

• exceptions;

• conflicts;

• expiry;

• conditions.

Monitoring tidak berarti setiap delegated action harus disetujui ulang.

⸻

26\. Delegation Review

Review diperlukan apabila:

• review date tercapai;

• material scope change terjadi;

• authority structure berubah;

• organizational boundary berubah;

• conflict muncul;

• repeated exception terjadi;

• material governance finding muncul;

• delegation tidak lagi diperlukan.

Review menghasilkan:

Continue

Modify

Suspend

Renew

Revoke

Escalate

⸻

27\. Delegation Renewal

Renewal bukan automatic continuation.

Renewal harus mempertimbangkan:

• original purpose;

• current need;

• authority validity;

• performance of delegated authority;

• findings;

• scope;

• organizational change.

Renewal harus menghasilkan updated record jika terdapat perubahan.

⸻

28\. Delegation Modification

Jika scope berubah:

Existing Delegation

↓

Change Request

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Authority Validation

↓

Approval

↓

Updated Delegation

Scope tidak boleh diperluas melalui informal agreement.

⸻

29\. Delegation Revocation

Delegation dapat dicabut apabila:

• expiry tercapai;

• purpose selesai;

• authority owner mencabut;

• recipient tidak lagi eligible;

• scope dilanggar;

• governance violation terjadi;

• organizational boundary berubah;

• authority model berubah;

• material conflict muncul.

Revocation harus memiliki:

• reason;

• effective date;

• authority;

• affected scope;

• record reference.

⸻

30\. Delegation Suspension

Jika terdapat immediate concern tetapi revocation belum final:

Active

↓

Suspended

↓

Review

├── Resume

├── Modify

└── Revoke

Suspension harus memiliki authority basis.

⸻

31\. Delegation Expiry

Delegation yang mencapai expiry tidak boleh digunakan lagi kecuali renewal telah effective.

Expired

≠

Active

Continuation setelah expiry harus diperlakukan sebagai new or renewed delegation sesuai applicable mechanism.

⸻

32\. Re-Delegation

Delegated Authority tidak boleh melakukan re-delegation secara default.

Re-delegation hanya diperbolehkan jika:

• explicitly authorized;

• scope memungkinkan;

• record dapat ditelusuri;

• accountability chain tetap jelas.

Canonical chain:

Authority Owner

↓

Delegated Authority

↓

Sub-Delegated Authority

Setiap additional level meningkatkan governance risk dan karena itu harus memiliki explicit basis.

⸻

33\. Delegation and Ownership

Delegation tidak mengubah ownership.

Ownership

│

└── Remains with Domain Owner

Authority

│

└── May be Delegated

Untuk PD-03:

Governance Domain

↓

PD-03 Ownership / Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Action

Delegated actor tidak menjadi owner atas Governance & Compliance domain hanya karena menerima delegation.

⸻

34\. Delegation and Accountability

Delegation tidak secara otomatis menghapus accountability.

Delegating Authority

↓

Final Accountability

sedangkan:

Delegated Authority

↓

Execution of Delegated Right

Detailed accountability relationship berada pada C4.

C3 hanya menetapkan preservation principle.

⸻

35\. Delegation and Decision Governance

C2 menetapkan decision governance.

C3 mengatur bagaimana authority untuk menjalankan decision-related action dapat didelegasikan.

Relationship:

C2

Decision Governance

↓

Decision Authority

↓

C3

Delegation Governance

↓

Delegated Decision Right

Delegation tidak mengubah decision governance requirements.

⸻

36\. Delegation and Authority Governance

C5 menjadi authoritative section untuk detailed authority rights.

C3 menggunakan authority model tersebut.

Relationship:

C5

Authority Governance

↓

What authority exists?

↓

C3

Delegation Governance

↓

Which authority may be delegated?

C3 tidak menciptakan authority category baru.

⸻

37\. Delegation and Accountability Governance

C4 menentukan detailed accountability.

C3 memastikan delegation mempertahankan accountability chain.

Delegation

↓

Accountability Preservation

↓

C4

C3 tidak menggantikan accountability matrix.

⸻

38\. Delegation and Review Governance

C6 menentukan detailed review mechanism.

C3 menyediakan object yang dapat direview:

• delegation validity;

• scope;

• usage;

• compliance;

• effectiveness;

• renewal;

• revocation.

Review conclusion harus mengikuti C6.

⸻

39\. Delegation and Escalation Governance

Jika delegated authority tidak mencukupi untuk menyelesaikan matter:

Delegated Authority

↓

Authority Boundary Reached

↓

Escalation

↓

Applicable Authority

Delegated actor tidak boleh memperluas authority sendiri untuk menghindari escalation.

C7 menjadi authoritative mechanism untuk escalation.

⸻

40\. Cross-Platform Delegation

Delegation yang berdampak pada Platform lain harus menghormati:

• Platform ownership;

• Domain Boundary;

• applicable authority;

• architecture authority;

• governance authority.

PD-03 tidak boleh mendelegasikan kepada pihak lain sebuah authority yang sebenarnya dimiliki oleh Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-03 Governance Authority

↓

Governance Requirement

↓

Delegated Governance Action

NOT:

PD-03

↓

Delegated Architecture Authority

↓

PD-02 Architecture Domain

⸻

41\. Architecture Decision Delegation

Untuk architecture decision, delegation harus mengikuti applicable Architecture Authority.

Jika PD-02 mendelegasikan architecture decision authority:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

↓

Architecture Delegation

↓

Delegated Architecture Authority

PD-03 hanya mengatur governance requirement yang berlaku terhadap delegation tersebut.

PD-03 tidak menjadi source of Architecture Authority.

⸻

42\. Governance Decision Delegation

Untuk governance decision:

PD-03

Governance Authority

↓

Governance Decision Authority

↓

Delegated Governance Authority

Delegation harus tetap berada dalam Governance & Compliance domain.

⸻

43\. Policy Delegation

Policy-related authority dapat didelegasikan jika applicable authority model mengizinkannya.

Delegation harus menetapkan:

• policy scope;

• permitted action;

• approval requirement;

• review requirement;

• expiry;

• record.

Delegation tidak membuat delegated actor menjadi policy owner secara otomatis.

⸻

44\. Standards Delegation

Standards-related action dapat didelegasikan dalam scope yang ditetapkan.

Contoh action:

• drafting;

• maintenance;

• review coordination;

• evidence collection;

• implementation monitoring.

Final authority terhadap standards tetap mengikuti applicable authority model.

⸻

45\. Approval Delegation

Approval authority hanya dapat didelegasikan apabila authority model mengizinkannya.

Delegation harus menetapkan:

• approval class;

• scope;

• threshold jika applicable;

• constraints;

• effective period.

Delegated approval harus tetap dapat ditelusuri kepada delegating authority.

⸻

46\. Certification Delegation

Certification-related activity dapat didelegasikan jika permitted.

Delegation harus membedakan:

Assessment

≠

Certification Decision

Pendelegasian assessment activity tidak otomatis memberikan certification authority.

⸻

47\. Compliance Delegation

Compliance activity dapat didelegasikan untuk:

• evidence collection;

• assessment;

• monitoring;

• corrective-action follow-up;

• verification.

Namun delegated compliance activity harus tetap berada dalam applicable governance framework.

⸻

48\. Delegation Materiality

Delegation materiality dapat mempertimbangkan:

• authority level;

• decision impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• reversibility;

• duration;

• strategic implication;

• compliance implication;

• architecture implication;

• potential conflict of interest.

Material delegation membutuhkan review yang lebih ketat.

⸻

49\. Delegation Risk

Risiko delegation dapat meliputi:

• authority overreach;

• scope ambiguity;

• accountability gap;

• conflict of interest;

• unauthorized re-delegation;

• stale delegation;

• expired delegation;

• inadequate evidence;

• cross-platform conflict.

Risk harus dikelola melalui:

• scope;

• constraints;

• monitoring;

• review;

• revocation;

• escalation.

⸻

50\. Delegation Independence

Jika delegated authority melakukan review atau assessment terhadap matter yang memiliki conflict of interest, applicable review independence requirement harus diterapkan.

Delegation tidak boleh digunakan untuk menghindari independence.

Delegation

≠

Independence Waiver

⸻

51\. Delegation Evidence

Material delegation harus memiliki evidence:

• authority basis;

• approval;

• scope;

• effective status;

• usage;

• review;

• exceptions;

• revocation / renewal.

Evidence menjadi bagian dari Delegation Register atau linked governance record.

⸻

52\. Delegation Auditability

Delegation harus dapat diaudit terhadap:

• who delegated;

• who received;

• what was delegated;

• authority basis;

• scope;

• duration;

• conditions;

• actions taken;

• review;

• renewal;

• revocation.

Auditability harus tersedia tanpa mengubah historical evidence.

⸻

53\. Historical Delegation Integrity

Historical delegation record tidak boleh diubah untuk menghapus:

• original scope;

• original authority;

• original approval;

• historical action;

• revocation history.

Jika correction diperlukan:

Original Record

↓

Correction Record

↓

Traceable History

⸻

54\. Delegation Exception

Exception terhadap delegation rule harus memiliki:

• exception ID;

• reason;

• scope;

• authority;

• duration;

• compensating control;

• review;

• closure.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent informal delegation.

⸻

55\. Delegation Failure States

Delegation governance harus mendukung state:

Draft

Pending

Approved

Active

Suspended

Modified

Expired

Revoked

Rejected

Escalated

Archived

Status harus merefleksikan actual governance state.

⸻

56\. Unauthorized Delegation

Jika delegation ditemukan tanpa authority basis:

Unauthorized Delegation

↓

Record Finding

↓

Suspend / Stop Exercise

↓

Authority Review

↓

Correct / Revoke

↓

Escalate if Material

Unauthorized delegation tidak boleh dianggap valid hanya karena telah digunakan sebelumnya.

⸻

57\. Delegation Scope Violation

Jika delegated authority melampaui scope:

Scope Violation

↓

Record

↓

Assess Impact

↓

Suspend if Required

↓

Correct

↓

Review

↓

Escalate if Material

Delegated actor tidak boleh memperluas scope secara unilateral.

⸻

58\. Delegation Register Governance

Delegation Register harus memiliki:

• unique identifier;

• current status;

• authority owner;

• delegated party;

• scope;

• effective period;

• related records;

• review status;

• revocation status.

Register harus memiliki single authoritative source.

⸻

59\. Delegation Change Control

Perubahan terhadap delegation mechanism harus mengikuti governance change control.

Material changes harus mempertimbangkan:

• authority impact;

• organizational impact;

• accountability impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• architecture implication;

• compliance implication.

Change tidak boleh dilakukan melalui silent overwrite.

⸻

60\. Delegation Governance Control Model

Delegation control menggunakan:

Preventive Control

↓

Authority / Eligibility Validation

Detective Control

↓

Delegation Review / Monitoring

Corrective Control

↓

Modification / Suspension / Revocation

Assurance Control

↓

Audit / Traceability

Control depth harus proporsional terhadap delegation materiality.

⸻

61\. Governance Interface

PD-03 delegation governance berinteraksi dengan:

Executive Authority

│

▼

PD-03 Governance Authority

│

├── Policy

├── Standards

├── Approval

├── Certification

└── Compliance

│

▼

Delegated Authority

Interface tersebut tidak menciptakan organizational hierarchy baru.

⸻

62\. Architecture Interface

Relationship dengan PD-02:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

│

│ Architecture Delegation

▼

Delegated Architecture Authority

PD-03

Governance Authority

│

│ Governance of delegation

▼

Governance Compliance

PD-03 dapat menilai governance compliance atas delegation.

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner delegated architecture authority.

⸻

63\. Cross-Platform Boundary

Delegation tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengubah:

• Platform ownership;

• Domain ownership;

• Architecture Authority;

• Security Authority;

• Quality Authority;

• operational ownership.

Jika cross-platform delegation diperlukan:

1\. identify originating authority;

2\. identify delegated scope;

3\. identify affected Platform;

4\. validate authority;

5\. validate boundary;

6\. record delegation;

7\. review material impact.

⸻

64\. Delegation Success Conditions

C3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. setiap delegation memiliki authority basis;

2\. delegating authority memiliki right to delegate;

3\. delegated authority teridentifikasi;

4\. scope eksplisit;

5\. constraints eksplisit;

6\. duration / termination condition tersedia;

7\. ownership tetap terjaga;

8\. accountability tetap dapat ditelusuri;

9\. delegation dapat direview;

10\. delegation dapat dicabut;

11\. Delegation Register tersedia;

12\. material delegation dapat diaudit;

13\. unauthorized delegation dapat dideteksi;

14\. scope violation memiliki handling;

15\. re-delegation dikendalikan;

16\. cross-platform delegation menjaga domain boundary;

17\. architecture delegation tetap berada pada Architecture Authority yang applicable;

18\. PD-03 tidak menciptakan Architecture Authority;

19\. delegation mechanism dapat diwariskan ke governance domain lain tanpa mengubah constitutional boundary.

⸻

65\. Canonical Delegation Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance governs the delegation of authorized governance rights through explicit scope, authority validation, lifecycle control, accountability preservation, review, traceability, and revocability, while preserving organizational ownership and the authority boundaries of Architecture and other Platform Domains.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance mengatur pendelegasian governance rights yang sah melalui scope yang eksplisit, validasi authority, lifecycle control, preservasi accountability, review, traceability, dan revocability, dengan tetap menjaga organizational ownership serta boundary authority Architecture dan Platform Domain lainnya.

⸻

68\. Canonical Boundary Rule

The following rule is normative:

Delegation grants a bounded right to act; it does not create ownership, constitutional authority, or authority beyond the delegating authority's valid scope.

Therefore:

Delegation

≠

Ownership Transfer

Delegation

≠

Unlimited Authority

Delegation

≠

Automatic Accountability Transfer

Delegation

≠

New Constitutional Authority

Governance Delegation

≠

Architecture Authority

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part C — Governance Architecture

\# C4 — Architecture Accountability Governance

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* C4

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Architecture Accountability Governance

\> \*\*Platform:\*\* PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Parent:\*\* C1 — Governance Constitution

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* C3 — Architecture Delegation Governance

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* C5 — Architecture Authority Governance

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Accountability Constitution

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Reference Implementation Adapted to PD-03 Domain

\---

\# 1. Purpose

C4 menetapkan \*\*Architecture Accountability Governance\*\* sebagai

Accountability Constitution bagi PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

Jika:

\- C1 menetapkan Governance Constitution;

\- C2 menetapkan Decision Governance;

\- C3 menetapkan Delegation Governance;

maka C4 menetapkan bagaimana accountability:

\- ditetapkan;

\- dipertahankan;

\- ditelusuri;

\- diverifikasi;

\- direview;

\- dipertanggungjawabkan;

dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

C4 memastikan bahwa governance authority, delegation, decision,

execution, review, stewardship, dan custody memiliki hubungan

accountability yang eksplisit.

\---

\# 2. Accountability Constitution

Accountability Constitution menetapkan bahwa accountability harus

dapat diidentifikasi secara eksplisit.

Setiap governance domain yang material harus memiliki:

\- Domain Owner;

\- Decision Owner jika terdapat decision;

\- Execution Owner jika terdapat execution;

\- Governance Owner;

\- Reviewer jika terdapat review;

\- Steward jika terdapat stewardship responsibility;

\- Custodian jika terdapat record/documentation custody.

Accountability tidak boleh diasumsikan hanya karena seseorang:

\- memiliki authority;

\- menerima delegation;

\- melakukan execution;

\- melakukan review;

\- memiliki akses terhadap artefak.

\---

\# 3. Accountability Position in PD-03

Accountability dalam PD-03 berada dalam Governance & Compliance

domain.

Model:

\`\`\`text

PD-03 Governance Authority

│

├── Governance Ownership

├── Decision Accountability

├── Execution Accountability

├── Review Accountability

├── Stewardship

└── Custodianship

Model tersebut tidak menciptakan authority baru.

C4 mengatur siapa yang accountable, bukan memperluas siapa yang memiliki authority.

⸻

4\. Accountability Objectives

AO-01 — Explicit Accountability

Memastikan setiap material governance responsibility memiliki accountable owner yang jelas.

⸻

AO-02 — Ownership Integrity

Menjaga ownership domain tetap konsisten sepanjang lifecycle.

⸻

AO-03 — Decision Responsibility

Memastikan setiap governance decision memiliki Decision Owner.

⸻

AO-04 — Execution Responsibility

Memastikan setiap governance action memiliki Execution Owner apabila execution dilakukan.

⸻

AO-05 — Governance Responsibility

Memastikan Governance Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap governance mechanism dalam scope-nya.

⸻

AO-06 — Stewardship

Memastikan domain dan artefak governance memiliki stewardship yang berkelanjutan.

⸻

AO-07 — Traceability

Memastikan accountability dapat ditelusuri melalui lifecycle aktivitas.

⸻

AO-08 — Auditability

Memastikan accountability evidence tersedia untuk review dan audit.

⸻

5\. Accountability Principles

AP-01 — One Domain, One Owner

Setiap domain organisasi hanya memiliki satu primary Domain Owner.

Shared responsibility tidak boleh digunakan untuk menciptakan ambiguous primary ownership.

⸻

AP-02 — Ownership Is Not Delegated

Delegation tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

Ownership

↓

Remains with Owner

Delegated Authority

↓

Bounded Right to Act

⸻

AP-03 — Authority Does Not Equal Accountability

Authority dan accountability merupakan konsep yang berbeda.

Authority

↓

Right to Act / Decide

Accountability

↓

Responsibility for Result

Memiliki authority tidak otomatis menjadikan seseorang accountable untuk seluruh outcome.

⸻

AP-04 — Accountability Before Execution

Material governance activity tidak boleh dilakukan tanpa accountable owner yang dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

AP-05 — Governance Independence

Governance accountability harus tetap dibedakan dari execution accountability ketika independence diperlukan.

⸻

AP-06 — Stewardship for Sustainability

Stewardship bertanggung jawab menjaga kualitas jangka panjang domain atau artefak yang berada dalam scope stewardship.

⸻

AP-07 — Traceable Responsibility

Seluruh material accountability relationship harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

AP-08 — Accountability Preservation

Delegation tidak secara otomatis memindahkan final accountability.

⸻

6\. AIOS Accountability Model

PD-03 menggunakan AIOS Accountability Model (AAM) sebagai reference model.

AAM memisahkan:

• ownership;

• authority;

• decision;

• execution;

• governance;

• stewardship;

• review;

• custody.

Model ini mencegah penggunaan satu role untuk menyamarkan seluruh jenis responsibility.

⸻

7\. Accountability Layers

￼

Matriks ini merupakan artefak turunan dari Accountability Constitution.

⸻

8\. Domain Accountability

Domain Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap integritas domain.

Domain Owner harus memastikan:

• domain memiliki accountable ownership;

• boundary domain diketahui;

• material governance responsibility memiliki owner;

• conflict ownership dapat diidentifikasi;

• accountability relationship dapat ditelusuri.

Domain Owner tidak otomatis menjadi:

• Decision Owner untuk seluruh keputusan;

• Execution Owner untuk seluruh action;

• Reviewer untuk seluruh matter.

⸻

9\. Decision Accountability

Setiap material governance decision harus memiliki Decision Owner.

Decision Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• decision context;

• decision quality;

• decision rationale;

• decision outcome dalam scope accountability;

• decision record.

Decision Owner tidak otomatis menjadi authority owner.

Relationship:

Authority

↓

Who may decide?

Decision Accountability

↓

Who is accountable for the decision?

⸻

10\. Execution Accountability

Setiap governance action yang memerlukan execution harus memiliki Execution Owner.

Execution Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• execution;

• completion;

• evidence;

• exception reporting;

• delivery against approved decision.

Execution accountability tidak memindahkan decision accountability.

⸻

11\. Governance Accountability

Governance Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap integritas governance mechanism dalam scope-nya.

Governance accountability mencakup:

• governance process;

• governance control;

• governance record;

• compliance mechanism;

• governance lifecycle;

• governance improvement.

Governance Owner tidak otomatis memiliki authority terhadap seluruh matter yang berada di dalam governance process.

⸻

12\. Review Accountability

Reviewer accountable terhadap:

• review activity;

• evidence assessment;

• review conclusion;

• identified findings;

• review record.

Reviewer tidak otomatis menjadi Decision Owner.

Review recommendation juga tidak otomatis menjadi decision.

Review

↓

Finding / Recommendation

Decision

↓

Authorized Decision Owner

⸻

13\. Stewardship Accountability

Steward bertanggung jawab menjaga kualitas jangka panjang:

• domain;

• knowledge;

• governance artefact;

• standard;

• record;

• reusable governance mechanism;

sesuai scope yang diberikan.

Stewardship tidak sama dengan ownership.

⸻

14\. Custodianship Accountability

Custodian bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• documentation;

• record integrity;

• historical record;

• retention;

• retrieval;

• traceability.

Custodian tidak otomatis menjadi owner atas substantive content.

Content Ownership

≠

Record Custody

⸻

15\. Relationship with Delegation

C3 menetapkan delegation governance.

C4 menetapkan accountability setelah delegation.

Canonical rule:

Delegation may transfer a bounded right to act, but does not automatically transfer ownership or final accountability.

Model:

Delegating Authority

│

├── Retains Ownership

└── Retains Applicable Accountability

│

▼

Delegated Authority

│

└── Accountable for

delegated execution

within scope

Detailed delegation mechanism tetap berada pada C3.

⸻

16\. Accountability and Authority

C4 tidak menggantikan C5.

Relationship:

C4

Accountability

↓

Who is responsible?

C5

Authority

↓

Who has the right to act / decide?

Authority dan accountability harus dapat dipetakan secara terpisah.

⸻

17\. Accountability and Decision Governance

C2 menetapkan decision governance.

C4 menambahkan accountability terhadap decision.

Relationship:

C2

Decision Governance

↓

Decision Process

↓

Decision Owner

↓

C4 Accountability

C4 tidak mengubah decision lifecycle yang ditetapkan C2.

⸻

18\. Accountability and Review

Review accountability harus dibedakan dari decision accountability.

Reviewer

↓

Assesses

Decision Owner

↓

Owns Decision Accountability

Review independence harus dipertahankan apabila review membutuhkan independence.

⸻

19\. Accountability and Escalation

Jika accountability tidak dapat dipenuhi atau terdapat conflict:

Accountability Issue

↓

Identify Owner

↓

Assess

↓

Resolve

↓

Escalate if unresolved

C7 menjadi authoritative mechanism untuk escalation.

C4 hanya menetapkan accountability condition yang memicu kebutuhan tersebut.

⸻

20\. Accountability and Cross-Platform Governance

Cross-platform relationship tidak boleh menghasilkan ambiguous ownership.

Jika satu concern melibatkan beberapa Platform:

Shared Concern

│

├── PD-03 Accountability

└── Other Platform Accountability

Masing-masing Platform tetap mempertahankan accountability dalam domain-nya.

Shared responsibility tidak sama dengan shared primary ownership.

⸻

21\. Governance vs Architecture Accountability

PD-03 Governance & Compliance dan PD-02 Architecture Office memiliki domain accountability yang berbeda.

PD-03

Governance Accountability

↓

Policy / Control / Compliance / Governance

PD-02

Architecture Accountability

↓

Architecture / Structure / Boundary / Consistency

Governance accountability tidak menjadi Architecture accountability.

Architecture accountability tidak menjadi Governance accountability.

⸻

22\. Accountability Boundary

PD-03 accountability mencakup governance concern dalam authorized scope.

PD-03 tidak mengambil accountability atas:

• architecture ownership;

• runtime execution;

• AI engineering execution;

• infrastructure operation;

• security operation;

• quality execution;

• developer enablement execution;

kecuali terdapat explicit governance responsibility yang memang berada dalam scope PD-03.

Governance interface tidak memindahkan domain accountability.

⸻

23\. Accountability Assignment

Accountability assignment harus menetapkan minimal:

• Accountability ID;

• domain;

• accountability type;

• accountable role;

• related decision;

• related authority;

• related delegation;

• effective date;

• review schedule;

• current status.

⸻

24\. Accountability Register

PD-03 harus mempertahankan Accountability Register untuk material accountability relationships.

Register harus memungkinkan penelusuran:

What?

↓

Which Domain?

↓

Which Accountability Type?

↓

Who is Accountable?

↓

Related Authority?

↓

Related Delegation?

↓

Effective When?

↓

Reviewed When?

↓

Current Status?

⸻

25\. Accountability Lifecycle

Accountability relationship mengikuti lifecycle:

Assigned

↓

Accepted

↓

Active

↓

Executed / Exercised

↓

Reviewed

↓

Verified

↓

Closed

↓

Archived

Perubahan material pada lifecycle harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

26\. Accountability Assignment

Assignment harus dilakukan sebelum accountability relationship menjadi effective.

Assignment harus memiliki:

• accountable party;

• scope;

• responsibility type;

• effective date;

• applicable condition.

Informal expectation bukan substitute untuk material accountability assignment.

⸻

27\. Accountability Acceptance

Accountable party harus dapat diidentifikasi dan menerima responsibility dalam applicable governance process.

Acceptance tidak berarti accountable party memperoleh authority baru.

Acceptance

≠

Authority Grant

⸻

28\. Accountability Review

Accountability harus direview ketika:

• organization berubah;

• domain boundary berubah;

• authority berubah;

• delegation berubah;

• material responsibility berubah;

• accountability conflict muncul;

• role tidak lagi eligible;

• review schedule tercapai.

Review dapat menghasilkan:

• Continue;

• Modify;

• Reassign;

• Suspend;

• Close;

• Escalate.

⸻

29\. Accountability Modification

Perubahan accountability harus terdokumentasi.

Model:

Existing Accountability

↓

Change Requirement

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Approved Change

↓

Updated Accountability

Historical accountability record tidak boleh dihapus untuk menyembunyikan previous responsibility.

⸻

30\. Accountability Exceptions

Exception dapat diberikan apabila terdapat:

• organizational transition;

• temporary role vacancy;

• emergency condition;

• structural change;

• approved Architecture Change Control.

Exception harus memiliki:

• reason;

• authority;

• scope;

• effective period;

• review mechanism;

• closure condition.

Exception tidak boleh menjadi permanent ambiguity.

⸻

31\. Accountability Conflict

Accountability conflict terjadi ketika:

• dua pihak mengklaim primary accountability;

• tidak ada accountable owner;

• accountability overlap material;

• authority dan accountability bertentangan;

• delegation menghasilkan ambiguity;

• cross-platform matter tidak memiliki clear accountability.

Handling:

Identify

↓

Classify

↓

Determine Applicable Accountability

↓

Assign / Clarify

↓

Record

↓

Escalate if Unresolved

⸻

32\. Accountability Gap

Jika tidak terdapat accountable owner:

Accountability Gap

↓

STOP ASSUMPTION

↓

Identify Applicable Domain

↓

Identify Domain Owner

↓

Assign Accountability

↓

Record

Tidak boleh mengisi accountability gap melalui asumsi informal.

⸻

33\. Accountability Overlap

Jika dua pihak memiliki overlapping accountability:

0\. identify primary domain;

0\. identify accountability type;

0\. separate responsibilities;

0\. assign primary accountability;

0\. document supporting responsibility;

0\. escalate if unresolved.

Model:

Primary Accountability

-

Supporting Responsibility

bukan:

Ambiguous Dual Ownership

⸻

34\. Accountability and Materiality

Review depth harus proporsional terhadap:

• decision impact;

• authority level;

• cross-platform impact;

• compliance impact;

• strategic significance;

• reversibility;

• governance risk.

Tidak semua accountability relationship memerlukan tingkat governance control yang sama.

⸻

35\. Accountability Evidence

Material accountability harus memiliki evidence yang memadai.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• assignment;

• acceptance;

• decision;

• approval;

• delegation;

• execution record;

• review;

• finding;

• closure.

Evidence harus dapat ditautkan kepada Accountability Register.

⸻

36\. Auditability

Accountability harus dapat diaudit terhadap:

• accountable party;

• scope;

• responsibility type;

• authority relationship;

• delegation relationship;

• effective period;

• actions;

• review;

• closure.

Auditability tidak boleh bergantung pada undocumented oral agreement.

⸻

37\. Historical Accountability Integrity

Historical accountability record harus dipertahankan.

Jika koreksi diperlukan:

Original Record

↓

Correction / Superseding Record

↓

Traceable History

Historical evidence tidak boleh diubah untuk menghilangkan historical accountability.

⸻

38\. Accountability and Governance Compliance

C9 menjadi authoritative section untuk governance compliance.

C4 menyediakan accountability object yang dapat diperiksa oleh C9.

C4

Accountability

↓

C9

Governance Compliance

↓

Compliance Assessment

C4 tidak menjadi compliance mechanism.

⸻

39\. Accountability and Review Governance

C6 menggunakan accountability relationship sebagai input review.

Review harus dapat menjawab:

• siapa accountable;

• apa scope accountability;

• apakah accountability dijalankan;

• apakah evidence tersedia;

• apakah finding memiliki accountable owner.

C6 tetap menjadi authoritative source untuk review mechanics.

⸻

40\. Accountability and Operating Architecture

Part D dapat menggunakan accountability model sebagai dasar operational responsibility.

Relationship:

C4

Accountability

↓

Part D

Operating Responsibility

Operating architecture tidak boleh menghapus accountability yang telah ditetapkan oleh governance constitution.

⸻

41\. Accountability and Performance Architecture

Part E dapat menggunakan accountability sebagai basis evaluasi performance.

Performance failure tidak otomatis mengubah accountability.

Performance Result

↓

Performance Review

↓

Accountability Assessment

Perubahan accountability tetap mengikuti governance mechanism.

⸻

42\. Accountability Independence

Jika accountable owner juga menjadi reviewer terhadap dirinya sendiri, independence requirement harus dievaluasi.

Delegation tidak boleh digunakan untuk menghilangkan required independence.

Accountability

≠

Automatic Review Independence

⸻

43\. Accountability and Stewardship

Stewardship memastikan continuity.

Domain Owner mempertahankan domain accountability.

Steward mempertahankan long-term quality.

Domain Owner

↓

Domain Integrity

Steward

↓

Long-Term Quality

Kedua role tidak boleh dianggap identik tanpa explicit assignment.

⸻

44\. Accountability and Custody

Custodian mempertahankan record.

Accountable Owner mempertahankan substantive responsibility.

Accountable Owner

↓

Substantive Responsibility

Custodian

↓

Record Integrity

Custody tidak memindahkan accountability atas substantive outcome.

⸻

45\. Accountability and Delegated Execution

Jika action didelegasikan:

Accountable Owner

│

└── retains applicable accountability

│

▼

Delegated Execution Owner

│

└── accountable for

delegated execution

Scope accountability delegated actor harus sesuai dengan scope delegation.

⸻

46\. Governance Accountability Matrix

￼

Matrix ini tidak menggantikan detailed assignment pada Accountability Register.

⸻

47\. Accountability Control Model

C4 menggunakan control layers:

Preventive

↓

Explicit Assignment

Detective

↓

Review / Monitoring

Corrective

↓

Reassignment / Clarification

Assurance

↓

Audit / Traceability

Control depth harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

48\. Accountability Failure

Accountability failure dapat terjadi apabila:

• owner tidak ditetapkan;

• owner tidak menjalankan responsibility;

• responsibility tidak dapat ditelusuri;

• execution dilakukan tanpa accountability;

• delegation menciptakan accountability gap;

• review tidak memiliki accountable owner;

• cross-platform responsibility tidak jelas.

Handling:

Detect

↓

Record

↓

Assess

↓

Assign / Correct

↓

Review

↓

Escalate if Material

⸻

49\. Accountability Escalation Trigger

Escalation dapat diperlukan apabila:

• accountability gap material;

• unresolved accountability conflict;

• domain ownership conflict;

• material governance failure;

• delegated accountability tidak dapat dipenuhi;

• accountable owner tidak dapat ditentukan melalui applicable governance mechanism.

Escalation mengikuti C7.

⸻

50\. Accountability Governance Records

Material accountability records minimal mencakup:

• Accountability ID;

• Domain;

• Accountable Role;

• Accountability Type;

• Related Decision;

• Related Authority;

• Related Delegation;

• Effective Date;

• Review Schedule;

• Current Status;

• Review History;

• Audit History;

• Closure Record.

Metadata tersebut membentuk Accountability Register.

⸻

51\. Accountability Status

Accountability relationship dapat menggunakan state:

Draft

Assigned

Accepted

Active

Suspended

Modified

Closed

Archived

Status harus merefleksikan actual governance state.

⸻

52\. Governance Interface

PD-03 menggunakan accountability sebagai interface antara:

Governance

↓

Decision

↓

Delegation

↓

Accountability

↓

Authority

↓

Review

↓

Escalation

C4 menjadi titik yang memastikan governance action memiliki responsible owner.

⸻

53\. Canonical Accountability Rules

Rule 1:

Setiap material governance domain memiliki primary accountable owner.

Rule 2:

Authority tidak sama dengan accountability.

Rule 3:

Delegation tidak secara otomatis memindahkan ownership atau final accountability.

Rule 4:

Execution harus memiliki accountable owner.

Rule 5:

Review accountability harus dibedakan dari decision accountability apabila diperlukan independence.

Rule 6:

Accountability relationship harus dapat ditelusuri dan diaudit.

Rule 7:

Cross-platform interaction tidak boleh menghasilkan ambiguous primary ownership.

Rule 8:

Accountability gap tidak boleh diselesaikan melalui asumsi.

⸻

54\. Accountability Success Conditions

C4 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. setiap governance domain memiliki Domain Owner;

0\. setiap material decision memiliki Decision Owner;

0\. setiap material execution memiliki Execution Owner;

0\. Governance Owner dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. Reviewer dapat diidentifikasi ketika review diperlukan;

0\. Stewardship dapat diidentifikasi ketika diperlukan;

0\. Custodian dapat diidentifikasi untuk material records;

0\. delegation tidak memindahkan ownership secara otomatis;

0\. delegation tidak menghilangkan final accountability;

0\. authority dan accountability tetap terpisah;

0\. accountability relationships dapat ditelusuri;

0\. Accountability Register tersedia;

0\. historical accountability dapat dipertahankan;

0\. accountability gaps memiliki handling;

0\. accountability conflicts memiliki resolution path;

0\. cross-platform accountability tetap menjaga domain boundary;

0\. material accountability dapat diaudit;

0\. review independence tidak dihilangkan melalui delegation;

0\. accountability changes dapat ditelusuri;

0\. model dapat diwariskan ke Platform Division lain tanpa mengubah prinsip constitutional dasarnya.

⸻

55\. Canonical Accountability Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance shall maintain explicit, traceable, and auditable accountability across governance domains, decisions, execution, review, stewardship, and custody. Delegation may assign bounded authority or execution responsibility but shall not, by itself, transfer domain ownership or final accountability. Accountability shall remain distinct from authority and shall preserve applicable governance, architecture, organizational, and cross-platform boundaries.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance wajib mempertahankan accountability yang eksplisit, dapat ditelusuri, dan dapat diaudit pada domain governance, keputusan, execution, review, stewardship, dan custody. Delegation dapat memberikan authority atau responsibility execution yang terbatas, tetapi tidak dengan sendirinya memindahkan domain ownership atau final accountability. Accountability harus tetap dibedakan dari authority serta menjaga boundary governance, architecture, organizational, dan cross-platform yang berlaku.

⸻

64\. Canonical Boundary Rule

Accountability identifies who remains responsible for a governed concern; it does not create authority, transfer ownership, or replace the applicable decision, delegation, review, escalation, or cross-platform governance mechanism.

Canonical model:

↓

Who owns the domain?

Authority

↓

Accountability

↓

Who is responsible for the result?

Delegation

↓

Who may act within delegated scope?

Review

↓

Who validates?

Escalation

↓

Who resolves an unresolved matter?

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part C — Governance Architecture

\# C4 — Architecture Accountability Governance

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* C4

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Architecture Accountability Governance

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* C5 — Architecture Authority Governance

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED
